# Medžuslovjansky matematičny kanon · Open Logic · OLP-0198

To jest polny aktualny kanon terminov, ktory jest použit v medžuslovjanskom izdanje Open Logic. Latinična forma jest redakcijno kanonična; kirilične formy sųt deterministično izvedene. Každy zapis pokazuje smysl, gramatiku, sravnitělne slovjanske formy, dokazy, alternativy, uvěrjenost i revizijnu liniju.

English: This is the complete current terminology canon used by the Open Logic Interslavic edition. Latin is the editable canonical witness; Cyrillic is deterministic. Every entry exposes its sense, grammar, Slavic comparanda, evidence, alternatives, confidence and revision lineage.

**Kontrolny punkt / checkpoint:** OLP-0198 · 400 aktivnyh zapisov · 453 fizične revizije · OLISV-T0346–OLISV-T0400 dodane od prethodnogo javnogo kontrolnogo punkta.

[Ekspertna knjiga odluka / expert decision log](EXPERT_REVIEW_LOG_OLP0198.md) · [machine JSONL](EXPERT_REVIEW_LOG_OLP0198.jsonl)

## OLISV-T0237 · algebra.abelian-group

- **English lemma / sense:** abelian group — A group whose binary operation is commutative, named here as an example of a many-sorted object domain.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Abelova grupa`; lemma `Abelova grupa`; feminine proper-name possessive phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive Abelovoj grupy; plural Abelove grupy. Preserve Abel as the eponym in citations.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Абелова група`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The direct mathematical group head combines with the officially described -ov possessive pattern for a one-word masculine eponym.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.86 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0237-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: абелева группа; uk: абелева група; pl: grupa abelowa; cs: Abelova grupa; bcs: Abelova grupa; Абелова група; bg: Абелева група.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[8040]; dictionary_id=1787; lemma=grupa; gloss=group); ISV-GRAMMAR-ADJ (lines 43-45; SHA-256 184348005ACB532868DAEB35DBFB7A8E05A493D550B4D6FA2E1A3BCFC906D84F); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** komutativna grupa: A valid semantic synonym, but it replaces the source's eponym rather than translating it.; abeleva grupa: Less consistent with the established project and official -ov possessive pattern.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete eponymic phrase is editorially composed; capitalization and national adjective vowels vary.

## OLISV-T0392 · algebra.cancellation-law-addition

- **English lemma / sense:** cancellation law for addition — The implication x+y=x+z entails y=z in Peano arithmetic.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `zakon skraćenja dodavanja`; lemma `zakon skraćenja dodavanja`; masculine noun with two genitive complements.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Zakon is masculine; skraćenja and dodavanja are genitive singular verbal nouns. The shorter contextual form zakon skraćenja is allowed when addition is explicit.
- **Kirilična forma:** `закон скрачења додавања`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Zakon skraćenja is transparent across East, West, and South Slavic algebraic traditions; the explicit dodavanja complement preserves canon T0366's distinction between the operation and its result suma.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.79 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0392-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: закон сокращения сложения; uk: закон скорочення додавання; pl: prawo skracania dodawania; cs: zákon krácení sčítání; bcs: zakon skraćivanja zbrajanja; закон скраћивања сабирања; bg: закон за съкращаване при събиране.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[10042]; dictionary_id=1901; lemma=zakon; gloss=law); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[15357]; dictionary_id=3139; lemma=skraćati; gloss=shorten, abbreviate); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[287]; dictionary_id=1704; lemma=dodavati; gloss=add); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic and standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** zakon poniščenja dodavanja: Poniščenje suggests inversion or annulment rather than cancelling a common addend from an equality.; zakon skraćenja sumy: Suma is the arithmetic result in canon T0387; the law concerns the addition operation, not shortening one result object.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The dictionary skraćati row is nontechnical; the algebraic sense is an evidence-linked editorial extension.

## OLISV-T0389 · algebra.commutativity-of-addition

- **English lemma / sense:** commutativity of addition — The property that interpreted addition has the same value after its two arguments are exchanged; the unit shows that Robinson Q does not prove it.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `komutativnost dodavanja`; lemma `komutativnost dodavanja`; feminine abstract noun with genitive complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use komutativnost dodavanja for the property. In predicate syntax use dodavanje jest komutativno. The genitive dodavanja follows the operation head OLISV-T0366.
- **Kirilična forma:** `комутативност додавања`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Komutativnost is already established in an accepted Interslavic algebra unit and aligns with direct Russian and Czech specialist evidence. The complement dodavanja deliberately follows current canon OLISV-T0366 rather than the older reader alias sčitanje.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.92 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0389-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: коммутативность сложения; uk: комутативність додавання; pl: przemienność dodawania; cs: komutativita sčítání; bcs: komutativnost zbrajanja; комутативност сабирања; bg: комутативност на събирането.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-MODELS-Q (lines 16-21, 113-118, and 172-173); OL-ARITHMETIZATION-CHECKING-DETAILS-ISV (lines 21-30 and 97-101); RU-MI-RAS-PROVABILITY-ARITHMETIC-2020 (physical page 2 / extracted lines 50-56); CS-CUNI-LOGIC-II-2008 (physical page 1 / extracted lines 23-28); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** komutativnost sčitanja: The property head is sound, but sčitanje is only a retained reader alias; current canon selects dodavanje for the operation.; zaměnimost dodavanja: This calque can suggest general substitutability rather than the exact two-argument exchange law.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Polish commonly favors a native property head, while East, West, and South traditions vary in their noun for addition; the edition keeps both the divergence and its current canon choice explicit.

## OLISV-T0186 · algebra.group

- **English lemma / sense:** group — A structure with an associative binary operation, an identity element, and inverses, here presented through a first-order axiom system.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `grupa`; lemma `grupa`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine noun; genitive grupy, plural grupy. In this formal context it names the algebraic structure, not an informal collection.
- **Kirilična forma:** `група`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Grupa is the exact frozen lemma and is transparent across all six comparison traditions; the current source fixes its algebraic rather than social sense.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.98 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0186-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: группа; uk: група; pl: grupa; cs: grupa (mat.); bcs: grupa; група; bg: група.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[8040]; dictionary_id=1787; lemma=grupa; gloss=group); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** skupina: The native alternative is common in Czech and some South-Slavic contexts, but it is less uniform across the six traditions and the dictionary explicitly supplies grupa for mathematics.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source gives a right-sided presentation of identity and inverse; the term remains the ordinary group concept under the displayed associativity axiom.

## OLISV-T0246 · algebra.homomorphism

- **English lemma / sense:** homomorphism — A structure-preserving map between algebraic or relational structures.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `homomorfizm`; lemma `homomorfizm`; masculine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive homomorfizma; use homomorfizmom after jest and homomorfizmy in the plural.
- **Kirilična forma:** `хомоморфизм`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The international Greek-derived form is unambiguous in algebra and is the natural name for the source's structure-preserving map. The record does not infer additional algebraic operations beyond the source's statement.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.82 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0246-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: гомоморфизм; uk: гомоморфізм; pl: homomorfizm; cs: homomorfismus; bcs: homomorfizam; хомоморфизам; bg: хомоморфизъм.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=16363; lemma=struktura; gloss=structure); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=1485; lemma=tělo; gloss=body); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** sȯhranitelj struktury: Descriptive but not the established mathematical noun.; izomorfizm: Too strong: the source has not yet established bijectivity plus inverse preservation.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No direct frozen homomorphism row was found in the bounded dictionary; national inflection varies.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0252 · algebra.ordered-field

- **English lemma / sense:** ordered field — A field equipped with an order compatible with its addition and multiplication.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `uporędženo tělo`; lemma `uporędženo tělo`; neuter adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive uporędženogo těla; use polno uporędženo tělo for the complete ordered-field phrase.
- **Kirilična forma:** `упореджено тєло`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Tělo is the established Interslavic algebraic carrier for field, while the ordering component follows the pinned oporędčeny family. The source's phrase is reproduced as a descriptive example, not a full axiomatization.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.7 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0252-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: упорядоченное поле; uk: впорядковане поле; pl: ciało uporządkowane; cs: uspořádané těleso; bcs: uređeno polje; уређено поље; bg: наредено поле.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=20002; lemma=oporędčeny; gloss=well-ordered); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=1485; lemma=tělo; gloss=body); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** uporędženo polje: Polje is a field component in several languages but is already used for field as a generic area and is less stable in the inherited canon.; linearna algebraična struktura: Too broad and omits the field axioms.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete phrase is editorial and the source does not list compatibility axioms; no stronger mathematical claim is made.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0366 · arithmetic.addition

- **English lemma / sense:** addition — The binary arithmetic operation interpreting + in the standard natural-number model and, by transported exponent addition, in the isomorphic one-letter-string model.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `dodavanje`; lemma `dodavanje`; neuter verbal noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use dodavanje for the operation; genitive dodavanja. At the first reader-facing occurrence retain sčitanje only as a Czech-facing explanatory gloss, not as a second canon head or unrestricted synonym.
- **Kirilična forma:** `додавање`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Dodavanje is a regular, transparent nominalization of the official Interslavic verb dodavati and is directly reinforced by Ukrainian mathematical usage. Because national arithmetic terminology diverges sharply, the first occurrence also gives sčitanje as a narrowly labelled Czech-facing gloss, without promoting the count/census family to a competing canon head.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.9 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0366-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: сложение; uk: додавання; pl: dodawanie; cs: sčítání; bcs: zbrajanje; bg: събиране.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-INTRODUCTION (lines 12-22 and 29-34); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[287]; dictionary_id=1704; lemma=dodavati; gloss=add); UK-VSPU-MATHEMATICAL-LOGIC-2007 (physical page 64 / extracted lines 2864-2874); CS-CUNI-LOGIC-II-2008 (physical page 1 / extracted lines 25-27); HR-ZAGREB-CLASSICAL-LOGIC-2013 (physical page 66 / extracted line 47); BG-SOFIA-PROLOG-ARITHMETIC-EXERCISE-4 (UTF-8 derivative line 60); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** sčitanje: Useful as a Czech-facing local gloss, but as a canon head or unrestricted synonym it privileges the West-Slavic count/add family and collides with the direct Interslavic count/census sense.; složenje: It closely follows Russian сложение but risks the broader senses composition or putting together and lacks the direct official Interslavic add verb.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The exact noun dodavanje is derived from a directly listed verb rather than independently listed as a specialist dictionary head.; The parenthetical sčitanje is only a Czech-facing explanatory gloss; it is not a canon alias because the same Interslavic family also denotes counting and census.

## OLISV-T0188 · arithmetic.induction-schema

- **English lemma / sense:** induction schema — The infinite family of induction instances in first-order arithmetic, one for each permitted formula rather than one second-order axiom.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `shema indukcije`; lemma `shema indukcije`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine head with genitive complement; genitive shemy indukcije. Use instance of the schema for one permitted formula.
- **Kirilična forma:** `схема индукције`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The phrase follows a near-unanimous international family and distinguishes an axiom schema from one axiom and from the earlier proof method structural induction.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0188-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: схема индукции; uk: схема індукції; pl: schemat indukcji; cs: schéma indukce; bcs: shema indukcije; шема индукције; bg: схема на индукцията.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14890]; dictionary_id=18814; lemma=shema; gloss=scheme); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** indukcijska shema: The adjectival order is grammatical, but the genitive phrase aligns more uniformly with Russian, Ukrainian, Polish, Czech, Slovak, and the accepted edition's induction terminology.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source itself warns that its displayed schema is simplified; parameters and side conditions are not fully stated.

## OLISV-T0393 · arithmetic.integer-average

- **English lemma / sense:** average (integer midpoint) — An element z with x+y=2z or x+y=2z+1; it lies between x and y and is the lower integer midpoint in the remainder-one case.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `srědnje`; lemma `srědnje`; neuter noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use invariant quoted head srědnje in the defining sentence; the quotation marks signal that the remainder-one case is an integer midpoint, not an exact field mean.
- **Kirilična forma:** `срєдње`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Srědnje is the official dictionary's exact mathematical noun. Quotation marks and the adjacent equations retain the source's useful but slightly loose average label without falsely claiming exact division in the odd case.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0393-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: среднее; uk: середнє число; pl: średnia; cs: průměr; střed; bcs: prosjek; просек; bg: средно число.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1473]; dictionary_id=12566; lemma=srědnje; gloss=average); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic and standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** srědnja vrědnost: The longer calque is unnecessary because the official dictionary directly supplies the neuter mathematical noun srědnje.; polusuma: Half-sum suggests exact division and is false in the remainder-one case unless a floor convention is added.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source calls the lower integer midpoint an average; quotation marks preserve that looseness rather than hiding it.

## OLISV-T0376 · arithmetic.language

- **English lemma / sense:** language of arithmetic — The formal first-order language L_A whose nonlogical vocabulary is used to formulate arithmetic structures and theories such as Q.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `jezyk aritmetiky`; lemma `jezyk aritmetiky`; masculine noun phrase with feminine genitive-singular complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive jezyka aritmetiky; locative v jezyku aritmetiky. Use L_A when the source names the formal signature.
- **Kirilična forma:** `језык аритметикы`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Jezyk aritmetiky is the regular genitive compound already patterned by jezyk logiky prvogo reda (OLISV-T0096). Its components are official dictionary heads, while Russian, Czech, and Croatian sources independently attest the specialist construction.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.96 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0376-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: язык арифметики; uk: мова арифметики; pl: język arytmetyki; cs: jazyk aritmetiky; bcs: jezik aritmetike; језик аритметике; bg: език на аритметиката.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-STANDARD-MODELS (lines 12-20 and 43-45); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[9987]; dictionary_id=541; lemma=język; gloss=language); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1093]; dictionary_id=21256; lemma=aritmetika; gloss=arithmetics); RU-MSU-GODEL-LECTURES-2020 (extracted lines 509-511); CS-CUNI-LOGIC-II-2008 (physical page 1 / extracted lines 23-30); HR-ZAGREB-GODEL-INCOMPLETENESS-2011 (printed page 4 / physical page 6 / extracted lines 172-178); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** aritmetičny jezyk: The adjectival form is understandable but less directly aligned with the strongest Russian, Czech, and Croatian genitive compounds.; jezyk L_A: L_A is a notation for this particular signature, not the lexical head needed for the dictionary and prose.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The compound is a project-house specialist head assembled from official components; no Interslavic standards body has certified it.

## OLISV-T0367 · arithmetic.multiplication

- **English lemma / sense:** multiplication — The binary arithmetic operation interpreting the multiplication sign on natural numbers and exponent multiplication in the isomorphic one-letter-string model.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `množenje`; lemma `množenje`; neuter verbal noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use množenje for the operation; genitive množenja. Keep množit as the corresponding infinitive when verbal syntax is needed.
- **Kirilična forma:** `множење`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Množenje is a transparent action noun from the official Interslavic množiti and coincides exactly with direct Croatian mathematical usage and Ukrainian stem structure. It avoids unnecessary international morphology while remaining recognizable beside Russian and Bulgarian prefixed forms and West-Slavic national terms.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0367-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: умножение; uk: множення; pl: mnożenie; cs: násobení; bcs: množenje; bg: умножение.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-INTRODUCTION (lines 12-22 and 29-34); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[11437]; dictionary_id=19858; lemma=množiti; gloss=multiply); RU-MSU-GODEL-LECTURES-2020 (physical page 1 / extracted line 39); UK-VSPU-MATHEMATICAL-LOGIC-2007 (physical page 64 / extracted lines 2864-2874); HR-ZAGREB-CLASSICAL-LOGIC-2013 (physical page 66 / extracted line 47); BG-SOFIA-PROLOG-ARITHMETIC-EXERCISE-4 (UTF-8 derivative line 63); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** umnoženje: The prefixed form follows Russian and Bulgarian closely but is less neutral across West and South Slavic than the unprefixed official Interslavic base.; multiplikacija: The international loan is intelligible to specialists but is less transparent to readers who recognize the widespread native množ family.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The preferred noun is a regular editorial derivation from a directly listed verb rather than a separate dictionary headword.

## OLISV-T0382 · arithmetic.nonstandard-number-in-structure

- **English lemma / sense:** non-standard number of an arithmetic structure — An element of an arithmetic-language structure unequal to the value of every standard numeral; the term foregrounds its numerical role while non-standard element foregrounds structure membership.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `nestandardno čislo`; lemma `nestandardno čislo`; neuter adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter singular nestandardno čislo; instrumental plural nestandardnymi čislami. Use nestandardny element when the source foregrounds membership rather than numerical role.
- **Kirilična forma:** `нестандардно число`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Nestandardno čislo closely follows the direct Croatian phrase and the official adjective and number heads. A separate entry is justified because Open Logic deliberately alternates number and element according to whether the numerical or structural role is foregrounded.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.96 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0382-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: нестандартное число; uk: нестандартне число; pl: liczba niestandardowa; cs: nestandardní číslo; bcs: nestandardni broj; bg: нестандартно число.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-NONSTANDARD-MODELS (lines 19-46 and 90-97); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[11792]; dictionary_id=37954; lemma=nestandardny; gloss=nonstandard); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[11893]; dictionary_id=17215; lemma=čislo; gloss=number); HR-ZAGREB-CLASSICAL-LOGIC-2013 (physical page 66 / extracted lines 69-72); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nestandardny element: Correct for the same object when membership is foregrounded, but less faithful where the source explicitly calls it a number.; bezkonečno čislo: Suggests magnitude alone and fails for the general structure-relative definition, which is stated through numeral values.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Some national sources prefer non-standard element or describe the non-standard part rather than using a standalone number phrase.

## OLISV-T0187 · arithmetic.peano

- **English lemma / sense:** Peano arithmetic — The first-order arithmetic theory using zero, successor, addition, multiplication, order, and an induction schema.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Peanova aritmetika`; lemma `Peanova aritmetika`; feminine proper-name noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The possessive adjective agrees with aritmetika: genitive Peanovoj aritmetiky. Preserve the mathematician's surname as Peano in citations.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Пеанова аритметика`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The direct international head is unanimous, and Peanova supplies a compact possessive surface familiar to West and South Slavic readers while the comparison table preserves East-Slavic genitive phrasing.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.91 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0187-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: арифметика Пеано; uk: арифметика Пеано; pl: arytmetyka Peana; cs: Peanova aritmetika; bcs: Peanova aritmetika; Пеанова аритметика; bg: аритметика на Пеано.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1093]; dictionary_id=21256; lemma=aritmetika; gloss=arithmetics); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** aritmetika Peano: The indeclinable surname phrase is transparent and strong in East Slavic, but the possessive adjective integrates more naturally into the selected Interslavic syntax.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source deliberately gives a simplified induction schema and does not state every parameter or side condition of a full presentation.

## OLISV-T0277 · arithmetic.power

- **English lemma / sense:** power — The exponent or degree to which a number is raised in an expression a to the b.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `stųpėnj`; lemma `stųpėnj`; masculine noun.

The Q suffix is retained because it distinguishes the formal theory from arithmetic as a subject.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** aritmetika Robinsona: The genitive-name construction is strongly supported in Russian and Polish-style usage, but the possessive adjective is more compact and directly attested in Croatian and Czech.; teorija Q: Useful after introduction, but too opaque as the dictionary head and it suppresses the established eponym.

## OLISV-T0398 · computability.primitive-recursion

- **English lemma / sense:** primitive recursion — The function-building operation defining a new function from base and recursive-step functions; in this unit it witnesses that successor, addition, and multiplication are computable.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `primitivna rekursija`; lemma `primitivna rekursija`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The compound keeps the active rekursija head, adds the directly dictionary-attested primitivny adjective, and is supported by direct Czech and Russian academic terminology. The translated prose states the logically safe direction: primitive-recursive arithmetic operations are therefore Turing-computable.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** sȯedinjenje grafov: Suggests a construction joining graphs, not an internal graph property.; konečnost grafov: Confuses connectedness with finiteness.

## OLISV-T0309 · logic.accessibility-relation

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** odnošenje sovmestnosti: Compatibility is one intuitive reading of R(p,q), but the source also uses reflexive, transitive, and universal restrictions; accessibility names the wider formal role.

- **English lemma / sense:** axiom of reducibility — The historically specific Principia axiom of reducibility, cited here without formulation among axioms that by most standards do not appear purely logical.

- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive aksiomy reducibilnosti. Reserve the compound for the Russell-Whitehead historical axiom rather than generic reducibility claims.
- **Kirilična forma:** `аксиома редуцибилности`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The historically specific name warrants a separate entry. Reducibilnost is the most transparent international compromise across the six traditions; the much stronger East- and West-Slavic native derivations remain visible as adverse comparison evidence.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.67 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0228-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: аксиома сводимости; uk: аксіома зведення; аксіома редукованості; pl: aksjomat sprowadzalności; cs: axiom reducibility; axiom redukovatelnosti; bcs: aksiom reducibilnosti; аксиом редуцибилности; bg: аксиома за редуцируемост.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1498]; dictionary_id=24180; lemma=aksioma; gloss=axiom); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14072]; dictionary_id=30601; lemma=redukcija; gloss=reduction); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** aksioma svodimosti: The East-Slavic form is precise but weakly transparent across West and South Slavic traditions and lacks direct Interslavic lexical support.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The frozen dictionary's redukcija row does not directly attest reducibilnost or the Russell-Whitehead sense.; Czech and Ukrainian historical terminology varies between international and native derivations.

## OLISV-T0116 · logic.biconditional

## OLISV-T0154 · logic.bound-variable-occurrence

The standard-Latin machine head svezano normalizes the directly listed svęzany component; etymological display remains explicit in the use note and continuous reader.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nesvezano pojavjenje prěměnnoj: Unbound is useful pedagogical paraphrase but defines the free side negatively and does not name the source's positive bound category.; obvezano pojavjenje prěměnnoj: Obvezano risks the obligatory or mandatory sense and is not the accepted edition binding verb family.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The dictionary adjective has the broader connected/related sense; Open Logic supplies the variable-binding restriction.; Standard-Latin svezano and etymological-display svęzano are two scripted surfaces of one edition head, not competing concepts.; No native or official certification is claimed for the complete specialist compound.

## OLISV-T0307 · logic.box-operator

- **English lemma / sense:** box operator — The modal operator Box, written □, asserting that its argument is necessarily true.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `operator neobhodnosti`; lemma `operator neobhodnosti`; masculine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine operator; genitive operatora neobhodnosti. The symbol is written $\Box$.
- **Kirilična forma:** `оператор необходности`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Operator neobhodnosti names the symbol by its semantic function rather than its typographic shape, while the source explicitly introduces Box and its necessary-truth reading. This avoids a reader having to infer what 'square' means.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.88 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0307-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: оператор необходимости (□); uk: оператор необхідності (□); pl: operator konieczności (□); cs: operátor nutnosti (□); bcs: operator nužnosti (□); bg: оператор за необходимост (□).
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-MODAL-LOGICS (lines 31-38); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=33109; lemma=neobhodimosť; gloss=necessity); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** operator kvadrata: A literal typographic calque is understandable but hides the operator's logical role and can be confused with a square-operation symbol.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National texts may call the symbol box, square, or necessity operator; the canon records the semantic choice and preserves the source notation.

## OLISV-T0328 · logic.causal-relationship

- **English lemma / sense:** causal relationship — A relationship in which one event, condition, or state functions causally with respect to another, as modeled by causal logic in the source.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** pričinno odnošenje: Odnošenje is viable for a formally defined binary relation, but the source uses the broader ordinary-language relationship sense, for which svęź is more transparent.

## OLISV-T0062 · logic.compactness

- **English lemma / sense:** compactness — The property that satisfiability of every finite subset guarantees satisfiability of the entire set.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `kompaktnost`; lemma `kompaktnost`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine abstract noun derived from kompaktny; use teorema kompaktnosti for the theorem.
- **Kirilična forma:** `компактност`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Kompaktnost preserves the internationally shared compact- root used across most Slavic mathematical traditions and forms a transparent abstract noun; the surrounding definition supplies the non-spatial logical sense, while the Polish divergence is recorded rather than concealed.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.69 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0062-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: компактность; uk: компактність; pl: zwartość; cs: kompaktnost; bcs: kompaktnost; bg: компактност.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[3759]; dictionary_id=33088); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** zgustjenost: A native density-style calque suggests physical packing or topological density and does not identify the finite-subset property stated by Open Logic.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The exact abstract noun and specialist logical sense are editorial derivations from a directly attested component, and Polish mathematical usage does not share the selected stem.

## OLISV-T0061 · logic.completeness

- **English lemma / sense:** completeness — The property that every semantic consequence is derivable in the selected proof system.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `polnota`; lemma `polnota`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine abstract noun; use polny for the corresponding adjective and teorema polnoty for the theorem.
- **Kirilična forma:** `полнота`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Polnota keeps the full/whole semantic core visible to readers across East, West, and South Slavic traditions and has already remained stable throughout the accepted completeness block; it is less morphologically marked than dictionary popȯlnosť and less dependent on an international loan than kompletnost.

## OLISV-T0378 · logic.converse-proposition

- **English lemma / sense:** converse proposition — For a proposition of the form p implies q, the proposition with antecedent and consequent exchanged, q implies p; it is distinct from the inverse and contrapositive and need not share the original's truth.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `obratno tvrdženje`; lemma `obratno tvrdženje`; neuter adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter nominative obratno tvrdženje; genitive obratnogo tvrdženja. Use the complement k with dative when naming the original proposition.
- **Kirilična forma:** `обратно тврджење`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Obratno tvrdženje composes two official Interslavic heads and follows the South-Slavic obrat- family. The Polish witness supplies the decisive semantic taxonomy, while Ukrainian and Slovenian independently attest converse-statement or converse-implication usage. The canon definition explicitly prevents collision with inverse and contrapositive.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.91 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0378-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: обратное утверждение; uk: обернене твердження; pl: twierdzenie odwrotne; cs: obrácené tvrzení; bcs: obratna tvrdnja; обратна тврдња; bg: обратно твърдение.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-STANDARD-MODELS (lines 52-57); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14417]; dictionary_id=18030; lemma=obråtny; gloss=reverse); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16219]; dictionary_id=1087; lemma=tvŕđeńje; gloss=statement, claim, assertion); UK-KNU-MODEL-THEORY-2023 (physical page 19 / decoded extract line 898; physical page 61 / line 3192); PL-AMU-MATHEMATICAL-METHODS-CONVERSE (physical page 2 / extracted lines 51-65); SL-LJUBLJANA-ALGEBRA-DISCRETE-MATH-2011 (printed page 21 / physical page 27 / extracted lines 898-905); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** protivno tvrdženje: This risks the inverse or contrary proposition, which the Polish source distinguishes from the converse.; protivopoložno tvrdženje: Opposite is semantically broader and does not encode exchange of antecedent and consequent.; obratna implikacija: Directly supported in Slovenian and suitable when the object is explicitly an implication; the source asks for the converse proposition, so tvrdženje preserves the source category.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The selected full phrase is compositional rather than a standalone dictionary headword.; Direct sources vary between proposition/statement and implication heads; this entry is restricted to the proposition sense used by OLP-0193.

## OLISV-T0375 · logic.empty-string

- **English lemma / sense:** empty string — The unique length-zero finite string in the free monoid of words over the one-symbol alphabet, used here as the interpretation of zero.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `prazdny niz`; lemma `prazdny niz`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine singular prazdny niz; genitive prazdnogo niza. Reserve prazdno množstvo for the set with no members; here the object is the identity string under concatenation.
- **Kirilična forma:** `праздны низ`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Prazdny niz extends the established technical head niz simbolov (OLISV-T0133) and keeps the object visibly distinct from prazdno množstvo (OLISV-T0190). The direct Ukrainian witness shows that empty word is also conventional; the edition chooses string because this passage explicitly presents finite strings and concatenation, while recording the word convention as an alternative rather than erasing it. The effective source now uses Lambda, matching the same Open Logic revision's explicit empty-string notation.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.82 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0375-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: пустая строка; пустое слово; uk: порожнє слово; pl: pusty ciąg; słowo puste; cs: prázdný řetězec; prázdné slovo; bcs: prazan niz; prazna riječ; bg: празен низ; празна дума.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-INTRODUCTION (lines 24-34); OL-SETS-IMPORTANT-SETS (lines 46-54); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6101]; dictionary_id=16335; lemma=pråzdny; gloss=empty, idle); UK-KNU-LOGIC-FOUNDATIONS-2020 (physical pages 15 and 44 / extracted lines 582 and 1880); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** prazdno slovo: Directly supported as a Ukrainian formal-language convention and acceptable as an explanatory synonym, but it would switch the passage's stated string ontology to word terminology and weaken continuity with OLISV-T0133.; prazdno množstvo: The source uses the empty-string symbol as an element and concatenation identity; an empty set has no elements and is a different mathematical object already covered by OLISV-T0190.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No direct pinned full-phrase national source in this packet uses the selected string noun.; The immutable OLP-0192 source uses the empty-set glyph, but the hash-bound effective source corrects it to Lambda using the project's own explicit same-revision notation; both states remain auditable.

## OLISV-T0173 · logic.extensionality

- **English lemma / sense:** extensionality — The semantic invariance principle that the satisfaction of a formula depends only on the common domain and on the interpretations and variable assignments for symbols and variables actually occurring in that formula.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `ekstensionalnost`; lemma `ekstensionalnost`; feminine abstract noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Decline as a feminine consonant noun: genitive ekstensionalnosti; use ekstensionalny for the adjective. Relevantnost may explain the source synonym but is not the preferred technical head.
- **Kirilična forma:** `екстенсионалност`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Ekstensionalnost retains the internationally recognizable extens- technical family and follows the frozen Interslavic dictionary's s-normalization, reinforced by Russian, Ukrainian, and Polish specialist comparanda. Czech, BCS, and Bulgarian z-forms remain explicit adverse evidence. Open Logic supplies the exact semantic-invariance definition, preventing the internationalism from floating as an unexplained label.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.78 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0173-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: экстенсиональность; uk: екстенсіональність; pl: ekstensjonalność; cs: extenzionalita; bcs: ekstenzionalnost; екстензионалност; bg: екстензионалност.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6583]; dictionary_id=37683; lemma=ekstensivny; gloss=extensive); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[9397]; dictionary_id=36332; lemma=intensivnosť; gloss=intensity); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** ekstenzionalnost: The z-form is strong in Czech, BCS, and Bulgarian and remains fully intelligible, but the pinned Interslavic dictionary normalizes the parallel extens- and intens- international roots with s, matching Russian, Ukrainian, and Polish specialist practice.; relevantnost: The source gives relevance as a secondary name, but outside this proposition it has broader pragmatic and proof-theoretic senses and therefore does not uniquely identify semantic extensionality.; razširjenost: A native extension-family calque suggests spatial widening or dissemination and does not transparently identify the model-theoretic invariance principle.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No direct Interslavic dictionary lemma for logical extensionality was found in the pinned response.; Russian, Ukrainian, and Polish favor s, whereas Czech, BCS, and Bulgarian favor z; the selected s is an editorial project normalization, not a claim that the alternatives are wrong.; The source's informal phrase size of the domain conflicts with the formal same-domain premise and is translated as the domain itself; the unmodified source wording remains preserved as adverse evidence.

## OLISV-T0156 · logic.free-for-substitution

- **English lemma / sense:** free for substitution — A term t is admissible for substitution for x in A exactly when no free occurrence of x in A lies in the scope of a quantifier binding a variable that occurs in t.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `možno podstaviti vměsto`; lemma `možno podstaviti vměsto`; invariable modal-infinitive predicate.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The canonical token omits a copula: use Term t možno podstaviti vměsto x v A. In an existing copular frame, t jest možno podstaviti vměsto x is also permitted. The predicate remains invariant with masculine term and feminine prěměnna.
- **Kirilična forma:** `можно подставити вмєсто`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The modal-infinitive predicate states admissibility without gender agreement and continues the accepted edition wording možno podstaviti ... vměsto. It avoids conflation with free-variable occurrence while preserving the exact capture-avoidance condition.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.76 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0156-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: можно подставить вместо; uk: можна підставити замість; pl: można podstawić za; cs: lze dosadit za; bcs: može se uvrstiti umjesto; може се уврстити уместо; bg: може да се подстави вместо.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** svobodny za: A literal calque is gendered and can be misread as the already canonical free-occurrence relation rather than substitution admissibility.; dopustimy za podstanovku: Semantically precise but gendered; it would require agreement changes across term and prěměnna contexts.; podstavlivy: A compact coinage with no attestation in the frozen dictionary or accepted edition.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The frozen dictionary does not attest podstaviti or the complete predicate.; The full specialist expression is an edition-level selection, not native or official certification.

## OLISV-T0163 · logic.free-logic

- **English lemma / sense:** free logic — A logical system in which singular terms need not denote an object in the quantifier domain and existential generalization therefore requires an existence premise.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `svobodna logika`; lemma `svobodna logika`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive singular svobodnoj logiky. Keep the source's technical non-denoting-term sense distinct from informal freedom or permissiveness.
- **Kirilična forma:** `свободна логика`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Svobodny is directly registered with complete six-language coverage, and the feminine agreement yields the conventional international specialist compound used throughout East and South Slavic traditions and transparently related to West-Slavic equivalents.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0163-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: свободная логика; uk: вільна логіка; pl: logika wolna; cs: volná logika; bcs: slobodna logika; слободна логика; bg: свободна логика.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[7316]; dictionary_id=1977); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** voljna logika: Voljny is possible in ordinary free or voluntary senses but is less continuous with the dominant specialist term and can suggest volition.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete specialist compound is compositionally rather than directly attested in the frozen dictionary.

## OLISV-T0153 · logic.free-variable-occurrence

- **English lemma / sense:** free occurrence of a variable — An occurrence of a variable in a formula that is not bound there by a quantifier occurrence; the status is relative to the containing formula.

- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive svobodnogo pojavjenja prěměnnoj. Svobodna prěměnna is permitted only as contextual shorthand; the formal property belongs to an occurrence relative to a containing formula.

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The occurrence-level head states the source's formal object exactly and composes three already established edition components. Svobodno continues the accepted OLP-0144 wording and is directly recognizable across East, West, and South Slavic comparison traditions.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.88 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0153-r0001`.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** voljno pojavjenje prěměnnoj: Voljno is broadly recognizable but more polysemous with voluntary, optional, or loose; it would also break the accepted svobodno wording without a semantic gain.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National traditions differ in the adjective used for free, but the formal occurrence-relative definition removes ambiguity.; No native or official certification is claimed for the complete specialist compound.

## OLISV-T0318 · logic.fuzzy

- **English lemma / sense:** fuzzy logic — A logic designed in the source to model reasoning about vague properties rather than only sharply binary classifications.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `razmyta logika`; lemma `razmyta logika`; feminine participial-adjective noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine logika; genitive razmytoj logiky and plural razmyte logiky.
- **Kirilična forma:** `размыта логика`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Razmyta logika transfers the shared blur metaphor used by several Slavic traditions while retaining the stable logic head. It remains distinct from nejasno svojstvo, so the name of the formalism is not confused with a merely unclear specification.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.82 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0318-r0001`.

- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-OTHER-LOGICS (lines 23-24); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=30337; lemma=råzmyti; gloss=blur); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=30338; lemma=råzmyvati; gloss=blur); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=8933; lemma=logika; gloss=logic); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nejasna logika: Nejasny directly serves the source's vague property, but calling the logic itself unclear would suggest defective exposition rather than graded truth or membership.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National traditions divide between fuzzy loans, sharpness metaphors, and blur metaphors; razmyta is a transparent edition preference rather than native certification.

## OLISV-T0129 · logic.implication

- **English lemma / sense:** implication — The implication connective, classically truth-functional and constructively interpreted as a proof-transforming procedure; in Kripke semantics it is forced at a world exactly when every later world forcing the antecedent also forces the consequent.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `implikacija`; lemma `implikacija`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive implikacije; use semantic consequence slědovanje for the model-theoretic relation.
- **Kirilična forma:** `импликација`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** International term converges strongly and remains distinct from canonical semantic consequence. The dedicated OLP-0179 unit now broadens the current sense while the earlier revision remains preserved as scope provenance.

- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: материальная эквиваленция; uk: матеріальна еквівалентність; pl: równoważność materialna; cs: materiální ekvivalence; bcs: materijalna ekvivalencija; материјална еквиваленција; bg: материална еквивалентност.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** odnošenje ekvivalentnosti: The existing mathematical relation term is broader and does not specify truth-functional material equivalence.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Some textbooks use logical equivalence for a semantic relation holding under every valuation rather than the object-language connective.

## OLISV-T0331 · logic.mathematical

- **English lemma / sense:** mathematical logic — The mathematical study of formal logical systems, listed by the source as one rubric under which the introduced systems may be studied.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `matematična logika`; lemma `matematična logika`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine logika; genitive matematičnoj logiky.
- **Kirilična forma:** `математична логика`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Matematična logika is a direct, transparent composition whose six-language counterparts are virtually uniform. The source distinguishes the disciplinary rubric from mathematical motivation, and the translation preserves both adjective uses.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.98 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0331-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: математическая логика; uk: математична логіка; pl: logika matematyczna; cs: matematická logika; bcs: matematička logika; математичка логика; bg: математическа логика.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-OTHER-LOGICS (lines 33-36); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=36602; lemma=matematičny; gloss=mathematical); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=8933; lemma=logika; gloss=logic); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** logika matematiky: A genitive phrase can mean the logic internal to mathematics rather than the established discipline of mathematical logic.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source does not define the disciplinary boundary; this entry preserves its broad rubric without attempting a narrower taxonomy.

## OLISV-T0151 · logic.minimal-formation-sequence

- **English lemma / sense:** minimal formation sequence — A formation sequence having the least possible length for its target formula or term; the minimizing sequence need not be unique.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `minimalna poslědovnost formovanja`; lemma `minimalna poslědovnost formovanja`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive minimalnoj poslědovnosti formovanja; do not imply uniqueness, because distinct minimizing orders may contain the same strings.
- **Kirilična forma:** `минимална послєдовност формовања`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Minimalna retains the source's formal minimization label, agrees with the feminine formation-sequence head, and is directly supported across all frozen comparison columns. The definition and following qualification preserve the distinction between least length and uniqueness.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.86 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0151-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: минимальная последовательность образования; uk: мінімальна послідовність побудови; pl: minimalny ciąg tworzenia; cs: minimální posloupnost tvorby; bcs: minimalni niz tvorbe; минимални низ творбе; bg: минимална последователност на образуване.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[11107]; dictionary_id=2916); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[7252]; dictionary_id=25511); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** najkratša poslědovnost formovanja: The paraphrase is extensionally close but replaces the source's named minimality notion and obscures continuity with standard mathematical minimal terminology.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Multiple minimal sequences may exist; the head denotes minimal length, not a unique normal form.; The complete specialist compound is compositional rather than directly dictionary-attested.; No official or native certification is claimed for the complete specialist compound.

## OLISV-T0300 · logic.modal

- **English lemma / sense:** modal logic — A logical system extending propositional or predicate logic with operators for necessity and possibility across possible worlds.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `modalna logika`; lemma `modalna logika`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine logika; genitive modalnoj logiky.
- **Kirilična forma:** `модална логика`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Modalna logika combines the directly attested modal adjective with the stable logic head and keeps the source's necessity/possibility distinction visible. The definition, rather than the international label alone, fixes the specialist sense.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0300-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: модальная логика; uk: модальна логіка; pl: logika modalna; cs: modální logika; bcs: modalna logika; модална логика; bg: модална логика.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-MODAL-LOGICS (lines 31-39 and 67-89); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=37338; lemma=modaľny; gloss=modal); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=8933; lemma=logika; gloss=logic); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** logika možlivyh světov: A descriptive possible-worlds phrase explains one semantic device but does not name the broader system, which also applies to predicate logic and alternative modal readings.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The dictionary's modal adjective is marked with a soft-l variant while the edition uses the internationally transparent modalna surface; no official specialist certification is claimed.

## OLISV-T0296 · logic.monotone-valuation

- **English lemma / sense:** monotone valuation — An assignment of propositional variables that persists upward along the Kripke order.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `monotonna ocěnka`; lemma `monotonna ocěnka`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine ocěnka; plural monotonne ocěnky, genitive monotonnoj ocěnky.
- **Kirilična forma:** `монотонна оцєнка`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Monotonna ocěnka pairs the direct valuation head with the source's quoted monotone qualifier; the future-world clauses explain persistence rather than relying on the everyday monotone sense.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.86 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0296-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: монотонная оценка; uk: монотонна оцінка; pl: wartościowanie monotoniczne; cs: monotónní ohodnocení; bcs: monotona valuacija; монотоно вредновање; bg: монотонна оценка.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-INTUITIONISTIC-LOGIC (lines 200-218); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=29019; lemma=ocěnka; gloss=mark, rating, score, valuation, estimation, assessment, appraisal, evaluation); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** postojanna ocěnka: Constant valuation would forbid legitimate growth of knowledge and is stronger than monotonicity.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete adjective-noun compound is not directly listed, and national texts vary between valuation, assignment, and evaluation heads.

## OLISV-T0314 · logic.necessitation-rule

- **English lemma / sense:** necessitation rule — The rule allowing one to infer Box A from A in the modal proof system described by the source.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `pravilo neobhodnosti`; lemma `pravilo neobhodnosti`; neuter noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter pravilo; genitive pravila neobhodnosti. The rule is quoted as iz A zaključite Box A.
- **Kirilična forma:** `правило необходности`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Pravilo neobhodnosti names the exact inference displayed in the source and keeps its semantic purpose explicit. The formal quotation remains unchanged, so readers can distinguish the rule name from the rule's notation.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.92 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0314-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: правило необходимости; uk: правило необхідності; pl: reguła konieczności; cs: pravidlo nutnosti; bcs: pravilo nužnosti; bg: правило за необходимост.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-MODAL-LOGICS (lines 67-77); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=3087; lemma=pravilo; gloss=rule); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=33109; lemma=neobhodimosť; gloss=necessity); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** pravilo operatora: The operator-only label does not say what inference is licensed and could refer to introduction or elimination rules more generally.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Different proof calculi impose side conditions on necessitation; this entry records the rule as stated in the bounded source, not every calculus variant.

## OLISV-T0304 · logic.necessity

- **English lemma / sense:** necessity — The modal status expressed by Box A: truth in every relevant possible world, or the corresponding logical notion in S5.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `neobhodnost`; lemma `neobhodnost`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine neobhodnost; genitive neobhodnosti. Use neobhodno as the neuter predicative adverb/adjective surface.
- **Kirilična forma:** `необходност`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Neobhodnost follows the direct necessity family and cleanly distinguishes the modal status from mere truth in one world. The source's Box explanation and S5 characterization provide the specialist boundary.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.95 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0304-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: необходимость; uk: необхідність; pl: konieczność; cs: nutnost; bcs: nužnost; bg: необходимост.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-MODAL-LOGICS (lines 28-35 and 79-81); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=33109; lemma=neobhodimosť; gloss=necessity, indispensability); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=33105; lemma=neobhodimy; gloss=necessary, essential); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nujnost: The short South-Slavic-oriented form is possible, but neobhodnost is directly evidenced and remains immediately recognizable in East and West Slavic contexts.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National mathematical registers divide between neobhodnost, konieczność, nutnost, and nužnost; no single national form is treated as authoritative.

## OLISV-T0293 · logic.nonlogical-axiom

- **English lemma / sense:** nonlogical axiom — An axiom expressing mathematical subject matter rather than belonging to pure logical calculus.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `nelogična aksioma`; lemma `nelogična aksioma`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine aksioma; plural nelogične aksiomy, genitive nelogičnoj aksiomy.
- **Kirilična forma:** `нелогична аксиома`

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** iracionalna aksioma: Would mean unreasonable or irrational and would destroy the source's logical-versus-mathematical scope distinction.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Outside specialist context nelogičny means illogical; readers should retain the definition and pure-logic contrast.

## OLISV-T0323 · logic.nonmonotonic

- **English lemma / sense:** nonmonotonic logic — A logic in which adding information can invalidate a previously licensed inference, as explained by the source's overturning clause.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `nemonotonna logika`; lemma `nemonotonna logika`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine logika; genitive nemonotonnoj logiky and plural nemonotonne logiky.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nepostojanna logika: Nonconstant or unstable logic is not the source concept; nonmonotonicity concerns loss of inferences under premise expansion.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The frozen general dictionary does not directly list the mathematical monotonicity adjective; the source and prior edition use supply the technical boundary.

## OLISV-T0330 · logic.obligation

- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The lemma spans duty, obligation, and commitment; the source context, not the bare word, fixes the logical reading.

## OLISV-T0305 · logic.possibility

- **English lemma / sense:** possibility — The modal status expressed by Diamond A, read as A holding in at least one accessible possible world.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `možlivosť`; lemma `možlivosť`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine možlivosť; genitive možlivosti. Use možlivo or može byti for predicative paraphrases.
- **Kirilična forma:** `можливост`

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** možnosť: The shorter form is common in some national traditions, but the pinned dictionary's možlivosť head and the edition's existing možlivy family make the longer surface explicit.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The dictionary gloss includes occasion alongside possibility; the source context is required to select the modal sense.

## OLISV-T0306 · logic.possible-world

- **English lemma / sense:** possible world — A world considered by the modal semantics as a point at which a formula may or may not hold.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `možlivy svět`; lemma `možlivy svět`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine svět; plural možlive světy and genitive možlivogo světa.
- **Kirilična forma:** `можливы свєт`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Možlivy svět transparently combines the direct possible adjective with the direct world head and keeps the semantic locus visible to readers across scripts. It does not assert that possible worlds are concrete entities.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0306-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: возможный мир; uk: можливий світ; pl: możliwy świat; cs: možný svět; bcs: mogući svijet; bg: възможен свят.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-MODAL-LOGICS (lines 26-29, 35-38, and 43-48); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=280; lemma=možlivy; gloss=possible); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=3137; lemma=svět; gloss=world); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** alternativny svět: An alternative-world phrase can suggest a competing actual history rather than the source's modal semantic point and is less aligned with the direct možlivy evidence.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Possible-world ontology is philosophically contested; the term names the source's semantic device and not a metaphysical commitment.

## OLISV-T0229 · logic.predicate-quantification

- **English lemma / sense:** quantification over predicates — Quantification whose bound variables range over predicates, used here to characterize the boundary between first-order and second- or higher-order systems.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `kvantifikacija nad predikatami`; lemma `kvantifikacija nad predikatami`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive kvantifikacije nad predikatami; nad governs instrumental plural here and keeps the quantified-over relation explicit.
- **Kirilična forma:** `квантификација над предикатами`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The operation is not a duplicate of the quantifier or predicate heads: its added content is the range of the bound variables. Nad plus instrumental plural makes that relation explicit and aligns especially well with the South-Slavic comparison forms.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0229-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: квантификация по предикатам; uk: квантифікація за предикатами; pl: kwantyfikacja po predykatach; cs: kvantifikace přes predikáty; bcs: kvantifikacija nad predikatima; квантификација над предикатима; bg: квантификация по предикати.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[13292]; dictionary_id=36370; lemma=predikat; gloss=predicate (logic)); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** kvantifikacija predikatov: A bare genitive can obscure whether predicates are quantified, quantified over, or merely associated with the operation.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The frozen dictionary lacks a direct kvantifikacija lemma; the operation head is an editorial derivation from established kvantifikator.; The Croatian dictionary comparison row renders logical predicate as tvrdnja, unlike the other traditions.

## OLISV-T0164 · logic.premise

- **English lemma / sense:** premise — A proposition or formula used as an input to an inference; here the extra existence claim required for existential generalization in free logic.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `premisa`; lemma `premisa`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive singular premisy; plural premisy. Reserve prědpoklad for an assumption when the inferential premise role is not intended.
- **Kirilična forma:** `премиса`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Premisa supplies a compact specialist head that is directly conventional in Czech and BCS and internationally transparent elsewhere. It avoids the false friend posylka, whose frozen dictionary sense is only a parcel, while distinguishing an inference input from a generic assumption.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.66 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0164-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: посылка; uk: засновок; pl: przesłanka; cs: premisa; bcs: premisa; премиса; bg: предпоставка.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[12354], wordList[1270], wordList[16643]; dictionary_id=5751,5324,11247); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** posylka: The Russian logical cognate is a false friend in the frozen Interslavic dictionary, where posylka means only a package or parcel.; prědpoklad: Directly attested for assumption, but broader than the inferential input role and less compactly conventional in the source construction.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Premisa is an edition-level specialist adoption rather than a frozen-dictionary headword.; Russian, Ukrainian, Polish, and Bulgarian traditions prefer different native heads.

## OLISV-T0144 · logic.proper-prefix

- **English lemma / sense:** proper prefix — A prefix strictly shorter than the complete string, equivalently one whose concatenation with a non-empty suffix yields the string.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `strogy prefiks`; lemma `strogy prefiks`; masculine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive strogogo prefiksa; strogy marks strict inclusion and must not be read as severity.
- **Kirilična forma:** `строгы префикс`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The canonical mathematical strictness adjective blocks the competing everyday own sense of vlastny while the source definition supplies the exact shorter-prefix condition.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.82 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0144-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: собственный префикс; uk: власний префікс; pl: prefiks właściwy; cs: vlastní prefix; bcs: pravi prefiks; прави префикс; bg: същински префикс.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16395]; dictionary_id=12676); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[13310]; dictionary_id=805); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** vlastny prefiks: Vlastny is conventional for proper in several traditions but also directly means one's own, creating avoidable ambiguity for a receptive interlanguage readership.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National specialist traditions often use cognates of vlastny/proper; the edition selects the less ambiguous strictness paraphrase.

## OLISV-T0149 · logic.proper-subformula

- **English lemma / sense:** proper subformula — A subformula of a formula that is not identical to the formula itself; recursively, it includes all proper subformulas of the immediate components.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `stroga podformula`; lemma `stroga podformula`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive stroge podformuly; the strictness adjective agrees with feminine podformula and reuses the canonical mathematical sense selected for proper prefix.
- **Kirilična forma:** `строга подформула`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Stroga reuses the canon's strict-inclusion adjective from proper prefix, making the exclusion of the whole formula explicit and avoiding the everyday own-sense ambiguity of vlastna. The source's recursive clauses supply the exact formal boundary.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.82 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0149-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: собственная подформула; uk: власна підформула; pl: właściwa podformuła; cs: vlastní podformule; bcs: prava/vlastita podformula; права/властита подформула; bg: собствена подформула.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-GRAMMAR-ADJ (lines 14-18); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16395]; dictionary_id=12676); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[13310]; dictionary_id=805); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** vlastna podformula: Vlastna is familiar as a translation of proper in several national traditions, but it can also mean one's own; stroga keeps the strict-subset relation transparent and consistent with the existing canon.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete compound is an edition-level specialist selection rather than a direct dictionary headword.; Some national textbooks prefer vlastna/sobstvena-type adjectives; that variation remains visible in the comparanda and rejection record.; No official or native certification is claimed for the complete specialist compound.

## OLISV-T0279 · logic.proposition

- **English lemma / sense:** proposition — A truth-apt claim represented by a propositional variable in the Kripke explanation.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `tvrdženje`; lemma `tvrdženje`; neuter verbal noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter tvrdženje; plural tvrdženja, genitive tvrdženja.
- **Kirilična forma:** `тврджење`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Tvrdženje conveys a truth-apt statement across the Slavic comparison and avoids presenting the dictionary's ordinary proposal sense as the logical noun. Propozicijsky remains the established adjective for the system and variables.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.83 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0279-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: высказывание; uk: висловлювання; pl: zdanie; cs: výrok; bcs: tvrdnja; исказ; bg: твърдение.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-INTUITIONISTIC-LOGIC (lines 200-210); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=1087; lemma=tvŕđeńje; gloss=statement, claim, assertion); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=5124; lemma=propozicija; gloss=proposal); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** propozicija: The pinned direct gloss is proposal, creating an avoidable false friend when the source requires a claim known to be true.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Some specialist traditions use international proposition forms; the edition deliberately separates the noun from the established propozicijsky adjective.

## OLISV-T0119 · logic.propositional-constant

- **English lemma / sense:** propositional constant — A zero-argument logical symbol with a fixed truth value.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `propozicijska konstanta`; lemma `propozicijska konstanta`; feminine noun phrase.

- **Kirilična forma:** `пропозицијска константа`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The international compound matches established edition usage and all national specialist traditions.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.8 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0119-r0001`.
