- **Alternativy / alternatives:** istinnostna konstanta: The descriptive alternative is transparent but less established than the source's propositional terminology.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No exact frozen dictionary row attests the complete compound.

## OLISV-T0280 · logic.propositional-language

- **English lemma / sense:** propositional language — A formal language of propositional variables and connectives interpreted by valuations or Kripke structures.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `propozicijsky jezyk`; lemma `propozicijsky jezyk`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine jezyk; genitive propozicijskogo jezyka. The dictionary język spelling is normalized to the edition's jezyk surface.
- **Kirilična forma:** `пропозицијскы језык`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The phrase extends the edition's established propozicijsky system adjective to the direct language head; the exact source context prevents the ordinary proposal reading.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0280-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: пропозициональный язык; uk: пропозиційна мова; pl: język zdaniowy; cs: výrokový jazyk; bcs: propozicijski jezik; исказни језик; bg: пропозиционален език.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-INTUITIONISTIC-LOGIC (lines 180-184 and 200-210); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=541; lemma=język; gloss=language); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=5124; lemma=propozicija; gloss=proposal); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** jezyk tvrdženj: Readable but inconsistent with the established propozicijska logika and propozicijska prěměnna families.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The full compound is source-defined rather than directly listed in the dictionary, and national languages vary between proposition and statement roots.

## OLISV-T0278 · logic.propositional-logic

- **English lemma / sense:** propositional logic — A logical system whose formulas are built from propositional variables and connectives.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `propozicijska logika`; lemma `propozicijska logika`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine logika; genitive propozicijskoj logiky.
- **Kirilična forma:** `пропозицијска логика`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Propozicijska logika is already the edition's stable, internationally searchable system label. The ordinary proposal gloss is retained as adverse evidence, so the source—not that gloss—fixes the logical sense.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0278-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: логика высказываний; uk: логіка висловлювань; pl: logika zdań; cs: výroková logika; bcs: propozicijska logika; исказна логика; bg: логика на съжденията.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-INTUITIONISTIC-LOGIC (lines 128-149); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=8933; lemma=logika; gloss=logic); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=5124; lemma=propozicija; gloss=proposal); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** logika tvrdženj: Transparent, but it would diverge from the established chapter label and much South-Slavic specialist usage.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Propozicija alone means proposal in the pinned dictionary; only the qualified specialist adjective is preferred here.

## OLISV-T0168 · logic.propositional-valuation

- **English lemma / sense:** propositional valuation — A function that assigns a truth value to every propositional variable and thereby determines the truth values of formulas.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `ocěnka`; lemma `ocěnka`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive singular and nominative plural ocěnky. On first use, the technical sense may be expanded as ocěnka propozicijskih prěměnnyh. Keep funkcija vrednovanja for the recursively extended evaluation function, not for the initial valuation itself.
- **Kirilična forma:** `оцєнка`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Ocěnka is selected because the pinned published dictionary directly lists that Interslavic lemma with valuation in its English sense inventory and exposes an unusually broad East, West, and South Slavic cognate pattern. The Open Logic definition restricts the polysemous general noun to the function assigning truth values to propositional variables. The edition keeps funkcija vrednovanja for the separate recursive extension from all formulas to truth values, preserving the source's distinction between valuation and evaluation.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0168-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: оценка; uk: оцінка; pl: ocena; cs: ohodnocení; bcs: ocjena; оцена; bg: оценка.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[10768]; dictionary_id=29019; lemma=ocěnka; English gloss includes valuation); ISV-GRAMMAR-ADJ (declension framework used by the edition; noun form checked against the published dictionary row); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** vrednovanje: Recognizable in several South and West Slavic traditions, but in the current edition it already denotes the recursively extended evaluation function; using it for both levels would erase a source distinction.; prisvajanje istinnyh cěnnostij: Transparent but long, and prisvajanje is already the edition's canonical head for first-order variable assignment; the expansion remains available as an explanatory gloss rather than the recurring token.; valuacija: Internationally recognizable but lacks the selected candidate's direct pinned Interslavic dictionary attestation and its cross-family cognate support in this evidence set.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The direct dictionary row is polysemous and does not independently certify specialist logical usage.; Polish, Czech, and parts of South Slavic specialist traditions may prefer derivatives of value or evaluation rather than the selected ocěn- family.

## OLISV-T0311 · logic.provability

- **English lemma / sense:** provability logic — A modal application in which Box A is read as A being provable and Diamond A as A being consistent.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `logika dokazlivosti`; lemma `logika dokazlivosti`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine logika; genitive logiky dokazlivosti.
- **Kirilična forma:** `логика доказливости`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Logika dokazlivosti makes the source's provable reading compositional from the direct proof family. The source's consistency gloss remains a documented application-level simplification rather than a hidden lexical expansion.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0311-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: логика доказуемости; uk: логіка довідності; pl: logika dowodliwości; cs: logika dokazatelnosti; bcs: logika dokazivosti; bg: логика на доказуемостта.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-MODAL-LOGICS (lines 59-60); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=7161; lemma=dokazati; gloss=prove); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=2041; lemma=dokaz; gloss=proof, evidence); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** logika dokazov: A proof-objects phrase does not express the modal property of being provable and is less aligned with the national provability forms.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The adjective dokazlivy is derived rather than directly listed in the frozen dictionary; the source fixes its modal application.

## OLISV-T0221 · logic.purely-logical-sentence

- **English lemma / sense:** purely logical sentence — A sentence using logical or equality vocabulary but no nonlogical symbols; not a synonym for every well-formed logical sentence.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `čisto logično izrečenje`; lemma `čisto logično izrečenje`; neuter adverb-adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter phrase; genitive čisto logičnogo izrečenja. Use izrečenje bez nelogičnyh simbolov as an explicit definitional paraphrase.
- **Kirilična forma:** `чисто логично изречење`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The adverb-adjective construction closely converges across the six comparison traditions and preserves the source contrast with sentences containing nonlogical predicates. Existing canon entries supply izrečenje and the opposed nelogičny simbol family.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.86 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0221-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: чисто логическое предложение; uk: суто логічне речення; pl: zdanie czysto logiczne; cs: čistě logická věta; bcs: čisto logička rečenica; bg: чисто логическо изречение.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[3445]; dictionary_id=582; lemma=čisty; gloss=clean, pure, chaste); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[10432]; dictionary_id=363; lemma=logičny; gloss=logical); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** samo logično izrečenje: Samo can mean merely or only and may be read as limiting the count or discourse status of the sentence rather than excluding nonlogical vocabulary.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Čisty is directly frozen only in a generic purity sense; the specialist meaning is fixed by the source's explicit absence-of-nonlogical-symbols boundary.

## OLISV-T0100 · logic.quantifier

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** ograničenje kvantifikatorov: Useful explanatory wording, but broader than guard-predicate relativization.

## OLISV-T0155 · logic.quantifier-scope

- **English lemma / sense:** scope of a quantifier occurrence — The corresponding occurrence of the immediate subformula governed by a quantifier occurrence inside a containing formula.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `obseg kvantifikatora`; lemma `obseg kvantifikatora`; masculine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive obsega kvantifikatora. The etymological TeX reader displays obsęg; oblast dějstvovanja remains an explanatory synonym, not a second canon head.
- **Kirilična forma:** `обсег квантификатора`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Obseg is the standard-Latin machine form of the directly listed scope lemma obsęg and gives a concise head recognizable especially in West and South Slavic. The exact source definition prevents the broad dictionary senses coverage and perimeter from leaking into the logical term.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0155-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: область действия квантора; uk: область дії квантора; pl: zakres kwantyfikatora; cs: obor platnosti kvantifikátoru; bcs: opseg kvantifikatora; опсег квантификатора; bg: обхват на квантора.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14910]; dictionary_id=28929); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** oblast dějstvovanja kvantifikatora: This established explanatory phrase is transparent and retained at first-use or pedagogical loci, but it is longer and less suitable as the repeated compact head than the directly listed scope lemma.; sfera kvantifikatora: The international noun is recognizable but remains a broad spatial metaphor and lacks the direct scope gloss carried by obsęg.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The direct lemma is broad outside logic; the source locator supplies the formal governance sense.; Standard-Latin obseg and etymological-display obsęg are two surfaces of the same term.; No native or official certification is claimed for the complete specialist compound.

## OLISV-T0255 · logic.relation-variable

- **English lemma / sense:** relation variable — A variable ranging over relations of a fixed arity in a second-order language.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `prěměnna odnošenja`; lemma `prěměnna odnošenja`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Plural prěměnne odnošenja; genitive prěměnnoj odnošenja. The arity is stated with a městnost adjective.
- **Kirilična forma:** `прємєнна одношења`

- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=35526; lemma=prěměnna; gloss=variable); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=1992; lemma=odnošeńje; gloss=relation, relationship, ratio); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** prěměnna predikata: Too narrow: the source ranges over arbitrary relations, not only predicates.; odnošeńny term: Loses the variable category and conflicts with the source's term/inference distinction.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National traditions alternate relation variable and predicate variable; the source's arity condition motivates the broader term.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0069 · logic.satisfaction

- **English lemma / sense:** satisfaction; satisfy — The semantic relation in which a structure, under a variable assignment where relevant, makes a formula true.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `zadovoljenje`; lemma `zadovoljenje`; neuter noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use zadovolnjati for the verb: struktura zadovolnjaje formulu; the participle is zadovolnjeny.
- **Kirilična forma:** `задовољење`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Zadovoljenje keeps the already established edition family zadovolnjati–zadovoljivy coherent and is immediately recognizable in East and South Slavic. The Open Logic definition narrows the general satisfy family to the structure-assignment-formula relation, while the strong West Slavic fulfil-family alternative remains explicit.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.68 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0069-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: удовлетворение; удовлетворять; uk: задоволення; задовольняти; pl: spełnianie; spełniać; cs: splňování; splňovat; bcs: zadovoljavanje; zadovoljavati; bg: изпълнение; изпълнявам.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14811]; dictionary_id=444; lemma=zadovaljati; gloss=satisfy, please); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14808]; dictionary_id=3318; lemma=zadovoljstvo; gloss=satisfaction); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** izpȯlnjenje: Strong in Polish, Czech, and Bulgarian comparanda, but changing roots would split satisfaction from the edition's established satisfiability family.; zadovoljstvo: Directly listed but primarily denotes personal contentment rather than the formal semantic relation.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No registered dictionary row directly attests zadovoljenje in the formal model-theoretic sense.; West Slavic and Bulgarian specialist traditions favor an izpȯln- or fulfil-family root.

## OLISV-T0070 · logic.satisfiability

- **English lemma / sense:** satisfiability — The property of a formula or set of sentences for which some structure, with a suitable assignment where relevant, satisfies it or all members simultaneously.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `zadovoljivost`; lemma `zadovoljivost`; feminine abstract noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use zadovoljivy for satisfiable and nezadovoljivy for unsatisfiable; do not collapse the property into zadovoljenje.
- **Kirilična forma:** `задовољивост`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Zadovoljivost preserves the edition's established satisfaction-family distinction: zadovoljenje names the relation and zadovoljivost the existential property. The compact form is especially transparent to South Slavic readers, while the recorded comparanda expose the competing fulfil-family tradition.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.66 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0070-r0001`.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** izpȯlnimost: Strongly motivated by Polish, Czech, Bulgarian, and Russian comparanda, but it would break the accepted edition's satisfaction-family continuity.; zadovoliteljnost: The directly listed adjective means satisfactory, not logically satisfiable.

## OLISV-T0224 · logic.second-order

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** logika vtorogo reda: The East-Slavic and Bulgarian alternative is recognizable, but the frozen drugy component and West/South convergence make drugogo the broader project compromise.

## OLISV-T0177 · logic.semantic-consequence-closure

- **English lemma / sense:** closure under semantic consequence — For a sentence set Gamma, the set of every sentence semantically entailed by Gamma; Gamma is closed when it already contains every such consequence.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `zatvorjenje po semantičnom slědovanju`; lemma `zatvorjenje po semantičnom slědovanju`; neuter noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Zatvorjenje is neuter; use genitive zatvorjenja. In nearby prose, zatvorjenje is permitted after the semantic consequence relation is explicit.
- **Kirilična forma:** `затворјење по семантичном слєдовању`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The longer head makes the closure operator's governing relation explicit and reuses the edition's established zatvorjenje head from OLISV-T0045. The source definition fixes this as semantic, not proof-theoretic, closure; ordinary prose may shorten the term once that relation is visible.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.76 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0177-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: замыкание по семантическому следованию; uk: замикання за семантичним слідуванням; pl: domknięcie ze względu na wynikanie semantyczne; cs: uzávěr vzhledem k sémantickému vyplývání; bcs: zatvorenje po semantičkom slijeđenju; bg: затваряне спрямо семантичното следване.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[3510]; dictionary_id=31523; lemma=zamknųty; gloss=closed); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** zamknjenje po semantičnom slědovanju: The zamk- family is strong in East and West Slavic, but the established edition closure head zatvorjenje is more transparent to South Slavic readers and avoids a needless canon split.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No frozen dictionary row directly attests the full specialist phrase.; National traditions divide between zamyk-/domk-/uzávěr and zatvor- closure families.

## OLISV-T0072 · logic.semantic-entailment

- **English lemma / sense:** semantic entailment — The relation Γ entails A when every structure satisfying every sentence in Γ also satisfies A.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `slědovanje`; lemma `slědovanje`; neuter abstract noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use semantično slědovanje where contrast with syntactic derivability is active; otherwise the short form slědovanje is sufficient.
- **Kirilična forma:** `слєдовање`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Slědovanje keeps the highly recognizable follow/consequence root, matches established edition usage, and names a relation rather than only its result. The optional semantično modifier prevents confusion with syntactic derivability where both relations occur together.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.77 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0072-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: логическое следование; uk: логічне слідування; логічне випливання; pl: wynikanie logiczne; cs: logické vyplývání; bcs: logičko slijeđenje; logička posljedica; bg: логическо следване.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[7134]; dictionary_id=1939; lemma=slědovati; gloss=follow); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[4036-4037]; dictionary_id=2327,18328; lemma=poslědstvo, poslědica); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6198-6199]; dictionary_id=35549,35550; lemma=povlěkati/povlěkti za sobojų; gloss=entail, involve); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** povlěčenje za sobojų: Dictionary-backed for general entail or involve but too periphrastic for a named binary metalogical relation.; semantična poslědica: Transparent but more readily names the consequent or result than the entailment relation itself.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The dictionary directly attests the root but not the formal abstract noun.; Polish and Czech specialist traditions favor a distinct vypływ or consequence root.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** teorija prostyh tipov: Changes simple as a property of the theory into a potentially restrictive subclass of types.; Churchova teorija: Drops the formal type-theoretic content and is not self-describing for new readers.

## OLISV-T0075 · logic.substitution

- **English lemma / sense:** substitution — The recursive operation that replaces every occurrence of x in a term by t, and—when t is admissible for x in A—replaces all free occurrences of x in a formula A by t without variable capture.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `podstanovka`; lemma `podstanovka`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use podstanovka terma t vměsto prěměnnoj x. In a term it replaces every occurrence of x; in a formula it replaces all free occurrences only when t možno podstaviti vměsto x without capture. Generic zaměniti remains explanatory prose.
- **Kirilična forma:** `подстановка`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Podstanovka preserves exact inherited mathematical usage and the accepted Open Logic introduction. OLP-0158 now distinguishes the two recursive branches precisely: all occurrences in a term, but all free occurrences in a formula under the free-for condition. This append-only revision corrects the earlier compressed sense without changing the preferred head.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.78 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0075-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: подстановка; замена; uk: підстановка; заміна; pl: podstawienie; zastępstwo; cs: substituce; záměna; bcs: supstitucija; zamjena; bg: подстановка; замяна.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14249-14253], wordList[16508]; dictionary_id=3810,3811,19420,19421,1627,36101; lemma=zaměnjati, zaměniti, zastųpati, zastųpiti, zaměna); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** zaměna: Directly attested and useful in explanatory prose, but too broad for the recursively defined operation and may also imply exchange or swapping.; substitucija: Internationally recognizable but unsupported as an Interslavic lemma in the registered witnesses and unnecessary given inherited podstanovka.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The registered dictionary does not attest podstanovka.; The inherited Noether uses document mathematical substitution but not this exact recursive first-order operation.; No standalone verb entry is created; podstaviti occurs within OLISV-T0156 and accepted explanatory prose.

## OLISV-T0174 · logic.subterm

- **English lemma / sense:** subterm — A term occurring as a constituent within a term or formula whose semantic value contributes compositionally to the value or satisfaction of the containing expression.

## OLISV-T0158 · logic.universal-instance

- **English lemma / sense:** instance of a universally quantified formula — The formula A(t), defined as the substitution result Subst(A,t,x) for an admissible term t, regarded as a syntactic instance of the universally quantified formula forall x A(x).
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `podstanovny slučaj`; lemma `podstanovny slučaj`; masculine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive podstanovnogo slučaja. Use only for the admissible substituted formula A(t), not for an arbitrary example or semantic model instance.
- **Kirilična forma:** `подстановны случај`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Slučaj is directly glossed with case and instance across the frozen dictionary, while podstanovny restricts it to the source's precise syntactic construction. The compound is more transparent across Slavic readers than an unattested international loan.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.64 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0158-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: частный случай универсальной формулы; uk: частковий випадок універсальної формули; pl: instancja formuły ogólnej; cs: instance univerzální formule; bcs: slučaj univerzalne formule; случај универзалне формуле; bg: частен случай на универсална формула.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[2999]; dictionary_id=1333); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** priměr: Priměr names an example and does not assert the formal substitution relation to a universally quantified formula.; slučaj: Directly attested and retained as the head component, but too broad alone to identify the admissibly substituted A(t) construction.; instancija: Internationally recognizable in some national traditions but unattested in the frozen Interslavic dictionary and less transparent across all three Slavic zones.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The frozen dictionary directly supports only generic slučaj, not podstanovny slučaj.; National traditions alternate between instance and particular-case terminology.; No native or official certification is claimed for the complete compound.

## OLISV-T0071 · logic.validity

- **English lemma / sense:** validity — A formula's holding under every interpretation admitted by the semantics: truth in every classical structure, or forcing at every relevant world of every intuitionistic Kripke structure.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `validnost`; lemma `validnost`; feminine abstract noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use validny for the adjective and distinguish validity from truth in one particular structure.
- **Kirilična forma:** `валидност`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Validnost extends an already frequent and unambiguous inherited mathematical adjective into the precisely defined logical property. It is broadly international and avoids the registered legal sense of pravomoćny; the Open Logic source keeps validity distinct from truth relative to one structure. The dedicated OLP-0179 unit now broadens the current sense while the earlier revision remains preserved as scope provenance.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.82 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0071-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: общезначимость; валидность; uk: загальнозначущість; валідність; pl: ważność logiczna; cs: logická platnost; bcs: validnost; valjanost; bg: логическа валидност; общовалидност.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-INTUITIONISTIC-LOGIC (lines 180-184 and 220-222); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[18216]; dictionary_id=37979; lemma=pravomoćny; gloss=valid, legally binding); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** pravomoćnost: The registered family is expressly legal and would misstate the logical sense.; občeistinnost: Compositionally suggests universal truth but is unattested and needlessly replaces the stable inherited international root.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The registered dictionary does not directly list validnost in the logical sense.; National traditions divide between international valid- and native universal-validity formations.; Earlier narrower sense wording remains preserved in the superseded revision.

## OLISV-T0073 · logic.variable-assignment

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** priděljenje prěměnnyh: The noun is directly listed for assignment but its South Slavic columns emphasize a task or mission, making the unexpanded short phrase less precise for semantic evaluation.; prirěčenje prěměnnyh: It occurs in earlier accepted proof prose but has weaker direct lexical support and is less recognizable to East Slavic readers; it remains adverse history rather than a silent synonym.

## OLISV-T0157 · logic.variable-capture

- **English lemma / sense:** variable capture — Unintended binding by a quantifier in A of a variable occurrence that was free in a term t before t was substituted into A.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `zahvat prěměnnoj`; lemma `zahvat prěměnnoj`; masculine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive zahvata prěměnnoj. Use zahvatiti or zahvaćati only when the unintended binding event is explicit; ordinary binding remains svezanje or svęzanje in reader prose.
- **Kirilična forma:** `захват прємєнној`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Zahvat gives the accidental binding phenomenon a distinct compact head and is directly supported as a take/capture component. Keeping svęzanje for ordinary binding preserves the source's distinction between legitimate scope and unintended capture.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.74 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0157-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: захват переменной; uk: захоплення змінної; pl: przechwycenie zmiennej; cs: zachycení proměnné; bcs: hvatanje varijable; хватање променљиве; bg: прихващане на променлива.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[2910]; dictionary_id=571); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[2911]; dictionary_id=2292); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[2912]; dictionary_id=19363); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[18234]; dictionary_id=35526); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** svęzanje prěměnnoj: This collapses accidental capture into ordinary variable binding and therefore loses the source's central warning.; uhvaćenje prěměnnoj: A possible South-Slavic-style capture nominal, but less continuous with the directly registered zahvatiti/zahvaćati/zahvat family.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The capture family also has physical seizure or takeover senses outside logic.; West and South Slavic specialist traditions are lexically split.; No native or official certification is claimed for the complete compound.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nepolna semantika: Negation loses the source's ordered strength relation and can suggest an incomplete or defective construction.; ograničena semantika: Would imply an arbitrary restriction rather than the established weaker semantic regime.

## OLISV-T0167 · logic.x-variant-assignment

- **English lemma / sense:** x-variant of a variable assignment — A variable assignment that agrees with a given assignment on every variable except that it may assign a different domain element to the distinguished variable x.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `x-variant prisvajanja`; lemma `x-variant prisvajanja`; masculine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Variant is masculine: nominative x-variant, instrumental x-variantom. Prisvajanja is a genitive complement. In the Latin head x remains the mathematical symbol; the definition states that the possibly changed value is assigned to x.
- **Kirilična forma:** `кс-вариант присвајања`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** X-variant prisvajanja keeps the source's compact, standard model-theoretic label, composes it with the already established OLISV-T0073 variable-assignment term, and uses the directly frozen masculine lemma variant. The definition makes the direction explicit: the possibly different domain value is assigned to x, while all other variable values remain unchanged.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.8 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0167-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: x-вариант присваивания значений переменным; uk: x-варіант присвоєння значень змінним; pl: x-wariant przypisania wartości zmiennym; cs: x-varianta přiřazení hodnot proměnným; bcs: x-varijanta dodjeljivanja vrijednosti varijablama; bg: x-вариант на присвояването на стойности на променливите.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[18235]; dictionary_id=17059; lemma=variant; partOfSpeech=m.; gloss=variant); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1008-1009], wordList[1014], wordList[18234]; dictionary_id=22021,22022,36496,35526); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** x-varianta prisvajanja: West and South Slavic comparison columns often use a feminine cognate, but the frozen Interslavic dictionary head variant is explicitly masculine; promoting varianta would contradict that direct local evidence.; prisvajanje različajuće se po x: Transparent as explanatory prose but too long for the repeated technical head and less exact about agreement on every other variable; the full definition already provides this explanation.; x-modifikacija prisvajanja: Replaces the source's variant relation with an unsupported modification noun and obscures that the original assignment itself counts as an x-variant.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete specialist phrase is an edition-level compound rather than a directly listed frozen-dictionary headword.; National comparison traditions differ in the gender and syntax of the variant component; those forms are editorial comparanda, not native certification.; The pinned official Cyrillic projection renders Latin x as кс; mathematical formulas themselves continue to preserve the TeX variable x.

## OLISV-T0201 · mereology.fusion

- **English lemma / sense:** mereological fusion — An object composed of specified parts in the source's informal supplementation principle; the unit does not provide a formal definition of fusion.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `mereologična fuzija`; lemma `mereologična fuzija`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The adjective agrees with fuzija: genitive mereologičnoj fuzije. In an established mereological context the short form fuzija is permitted.
- **Kirilična forma:** `мереологична фузија`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Mereologična fuzija retains the source's international technical label and is especially transparent in West and South Slavic traditions. The qualification prevents confusion with physical, organizational, or biological fusion.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.78 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0201-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: мереологическое слияние; uk: мереологічне злиття; pl: fuzja mereologiczna; cs: mereologická fúze; bcs: mereološka fuzija; мереолошка фузија; bg: мереологична фузия.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[7467]; dictionary_id=35830; lemma=fuzija; gloss=fusion); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** slivanje česti: The native merger phrase is strong for Russian and Ukrainian readers but can describe a process rather than the mereological object or operation and is less uniform across the comparison set.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source does not define overlap or fusion formally.; Mereological fusion is not silently collapsed into the separately displayed least-upper-bound sum in this unit.

## OLISV-T0200 · mereology.mereological-sum

- **English lemma / sense:** mereological sum — The displayed least upper bound of two objects under parthood: an object containing both as parts and minimal with that property.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `mereologična suma`; lemma `mereologična suma`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The adjective agrees with suma: genitive mereologičnoj sumy; plural mereologične sumy.
- **Kirilična forma:** `мереологична сума`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The international compound is transparent and preserves the displayed order-theoretic definition. It is kept distinct from the later informal fusion term because the source does not define fusion or prove equivalence there.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** suma česti: The native complement is transparent but can suggest an arithmetic total of parts; the mereological adjective marks the technical order-theoretic operation more reliably.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source's later word fusion is informal and undefined; no equivalence with the displayed sum is assumed in the canon.

## OLISV-T0196 · mereology.parthood

- **English lemma / sense:** parthood — The relation holding when one object is a part of another; in the displayed basic theory it is axiomatized as a partial order.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `odnošenje česti`; lemma `odnošenje česti`; neuter noun phrase with genitive.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter head; genitive odnošenja česti. In prose use x jest čestju y for x is part of y.
- **Kirilična forma:** `одношење чести`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The compound uses two direct heads and makes the abstract English suffix transparent. It also provides a stable base for parthood structure, proper part, and improper part.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.76 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0196-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: отношение части; uk: відношення частини; pl: relacja bycia częścią; cs: vztah části; bcs: odnos dijela; однос дела; bg: отношение на част.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14174]; dictionary_id=1992; lemma=odnošeńje; gloss=relation); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[12471]; dictionary_id=1731; lemma=čęsť; gloss=part); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** čestnost: A compact abstract derivative would collide with the established honor/honesty family and would be opaque or misleading to many Slavic readers.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The frozen part row is marked weaker for several East and South Slavic columns; del/dio/частина remain important comparanda.

- **Medžuslovjansky:** `struktura česti`; lemma `struktura česti`; feminine noun phrase with genitive.

## OLISV-T0198 · mereology.proper-part

- **English lemma / sense:** proper part — A part of an object that is not identical with the whole object.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `vlastna čest`; lemma `vlastna čest`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The adjective agrees with čest: genitive vlastnoj česti; plural vlastne česti. It is the strict, non-reflexive portion of parthood.
- **Kirilična forma:** `властна чест`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Vlastna čest aligns strongly with Ukrainian, Polish, Czech, and Slovak proper-part traditions and remains reconstructible through own/proper senses elsewhere. The source fixes the non-identity boundary.

- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[12327]; dictionary_id=1913; lemma=vlastny; gloss=own); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[13541]; dictionary_id=22480; lemma=vlastny; gloss=proper); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[12471]; dictionary_id=1731; lemma=čęsť; gloss=part); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** stroga čest: The strict-order calque is formally transparent but less idiomatic across the comparison traditions and risks suggesting severity rather than non-identity outside formal context.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The frozen proper adjective row primarily means suitable or appropriate, so the technical non-identity sense depends on the formal source definition.

## OLISV-T0356 · model-theory.back-and-forth-property

- **English lemma / sense:** back-and-forth property — The extension condition on a nonempty family of partial isomorphisms: every element of either structure can be added on the corresponding domain or range side.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `svojstvo prodolženja naprěd i nazad`; lemma `svojstvo prodolženja naprěd i nazad`; neuter noun phrase with genitive complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter head svojstvo; genitive svojstva. The paired operational labels are Naprěd and Nazad. Prodolženje names extension of a partial map, not temporal continuation.
- **Kirilična forma:** `својство продолжења напрєд и назад`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Svojstvo prodolženja naprěd i nazad makes the formal action explicit: members of the partial-isomorphism family must be extendible on the domain side and on the range side. The directional words are exceptionally transparent across the dictionary's comparison columns. Polish supplies native paired labels, while Russian and Croatian retain English in academic use, so the full phrase is deliberately presented as an evidence-based Interslavic synthesis rather than a claimed pan-Slavic standard.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.78 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0356-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: метод back-and-forth; продолжение частичных изоморфизмов; uk: властивість продовження вперед і назад; pl: warunki Tam i Z powrotem; cs: vlastnost rozšíření vpřed a zpět; bcs: uvjeti (forth) i (back); bg: свойство за продължаване напред и назад.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM (lines 40-54); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=22019 svojstvo; 4385 prodȯlžeńje; 2791 naprěd; 2781 nazad); PL-UW-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical/printed PDF page 2, Definition 4); HR-ZAGREB-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical PDF page 21, printed page 13); RU-HSE-BACK-AND-FORTH-2012 (HTML line 47, Lecture 1); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** svojstvo naprěd i nazad: The shorter calque is readable but omits the decisive operation—extension of a partial isomorphism—and is therefore less informative out of context.; metod back-and-forth: The borrowed English term is attested in Russian and Croatian academic contexts, but it impedes readers who do not know English and hides the operation that the definition performs.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No single native specialist phrase converges across the pinned East-, West-, and South-Slavic sources.; Prodolženje is used in its mathematical extension sense; some readers may prefer razširenje.

## OLISV-T0345 · model-theory.basics

- **English lemma / sense:** basics of model theory — The introductory chapter that gathers the foundational topics and prerequisites for the model-theory material.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `osnovy teorije modelov`; lemma `osnova`; noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Osnovy is the nominative plural of feminine osnova ‘basis, base’; teorije is genitive singular of teorija, and modelov is genitive plural of model. Use Osnovy teorije modelov as the chapter title.
- **Kirilična forma:** `основы теорије моделов`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Osnova is the directly pinned Interslavic basis/base noun, and its nominative plural osnovy is the natural chapter-heading form for foundational material. Teorije modelov reuses the already accepted model-theory inflection, yielding a transparent title across Slavic readers.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0345-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: основа, база; uk: основа, підстава, база; pl: podstawa, baza; cs: základ, základna; bcs: osnova, baza, temelj; bg: основа, база.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-BASICS (line 8; \olchapter{mod}{bas}{Basics of Model Theory}); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1719]; dictionary_id=2049; lemma=osnova; gloss=basis, base); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1711]; dictionary_id=2831; lemma=osnovny; gloss=basic, fundamental); ISV-GRAMMAR-ADJ (lines 14-18; agreement and nominal inflection); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** bazis teorije modelov: Bazis is intelligible in some Slavic usage but is not the pinned basic-dictionary head for the general basis/base sense and narrows the pan-Slavic root convergence.; fundamenty teorije modelov: Fundamenty foregrounds a physical foundation and is less suitable for an introductory chapter whose contents are conceptual prerequisites and definitions.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The title is an editorial chapter-heading choice; future corpus context may distinguish osnovy from more specialized foundational terminology.

## OLISV-T0363 · model-theory.cantor-dense-order-isomorphism-theorem

- **English lemma / sense:** Cantor's isomorphism theorem for dense linear orders — The uniqueness up to order isomorphism of countable dense linear orders without endpoints.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Kantorova teorema o izomorfizmu gųstyh linearnyh poredkov`; lemma `Kantorova teorema o izomorfizmu gųstyh linearnyh poredkov`; feminine proper-name noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine adjective-noun agreement; genitive Kantorovoj teoremy. Keep the descriptive complement because bare Kantorova teorema is already ambiguous with the power-set theorem.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Канторова теорема о изоморфизму густых линеарных поредков`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The descriptive complement disambiguates this theorem from the power-set result already recorded as OLISV-T0217. Kantorova preserves the canon's deterministic K-to-К script policy, while the remainder composes already established izomorfizm, gųsty, linearny, and poredok heads. Polish directly supplies the Cantor attribution; Russian and Croatian independently fix the proposition and its construction.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.89 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0363-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: теорема Кантора об изоморфизме счётных плотных линейных порядков; uk: теорема Кантора про ізоморфізм зліченних щільних лінійних порядків; pl: twierdzenie Cantora o izomorfizmie przeliczalnych gęstych porządków liniowych; cs: Cantorova věta o izomorfismu spočetných hustých lineárních uspořádání; bcs: Cantorov teorem o izomorfizmu prebrojivih gustih linearnih poredaka; bg: теорема на Кантор за изоморфизма на изброими гъсти линейни наредби.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER (lines 28-60); PL-PW-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2022 (physical/printed PDF page 23, Zadanie 181 (Cantor)); RU-MI-RAS-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2015 (physical PDF page 12, problem 10); HR-ZAGREB-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2015 (physical PDF pages 52-53, printed pages 48-49); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/dist/index.browser.min.js; isv-Cyrl)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** Kantorova teorema: The bare eponym is ambiguous with Cantor's power-set theorem and therefore cannot serve as the machine-indexed canonical head for this result.; teorema Cantora o racionalnom poredku: This foregrounds only the canonical Q representative and obscures the uniqueness theorem for every countable dense no-endpoints order.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Only the Polish bounded witness explicitly prints Cantor's name beside this exact theorem; the Russian and Croatian witnesses support the theorem substance independently.; The long descriptive head is intentional for disambiguation; shorter prose may use the theorem label only after the referent is locally clear.

- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=35597; lemma=kategoričny; gloss=categorical); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=20000; lemma=opis; gloss=description); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)

- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The short phrase is context-dependent outside model theory; the source locator fixes its formal sense.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0354 · model-theory.complete-theory

- **English lemma / sense:** complete theory — A theory that decides every sentence of its language: for each sentence, the theory entails that sentence or its negation.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `polna teorija`; lemma `polna teorija`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The adjective polny agrees with teorija: nominative singular polna teorija, genitive singular polnoj teorije. This is theory completeness by sentence-decision, not completeness of a proof calculus.
- **Kirilična forma:** `полна теорија`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Polna teorija keeps the native full/complete family visible across Russian, Polish, Serbian, Ukrainian, Czech, and Bulgarian comparison evidence and remains continuous with the established canon adjective polny under OLISV-T0061. A separate entry is necessary because this property means deciding every sentence, whereas OLISV-T0061 names completeness of a proof system relative to semantic consequence. The Serbian source makes that distinction on adjacent pages, so identical surface morphology does not erase the polysemy boundary.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0354-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: полная теория; uk: повна теорія; pl: teoria zupełna; cs: úplná teorie; bcs: kompletna ili potpuna teorija; комплетна или потпуна теорија; bg: пълна теория.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-THEORY-M (lines 29-53); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[3816]; dictionary_id=10923; popȯlny; and wordList[7429]; dictionary_id=3141; pȯlny); RU-MSU-COMPLETE-THEORY-2026 (PDF slides 6-11); PL-AMU-COMPLETE-THEORY-2020 (physical PDF pages 25-26, Theorem 1.11); SR-BELGRADE-COMPLETE-THEORY (physical PDF pages 47-49, printed pages 39-41); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** kompletna teorija: The international adjective is directly attested as a Serbian synonym and is broadly recognizable, but it is less continuous with the established edition family polnota/polny and less transparent to readers relying on the native full/whole cognate family.; teorija, ktora odlučaje vsako izrečenje: This explanatory clause is useful in the definition but too long for the recurring canonical head and would obscure correspondence with the standard Slavic specialist terminology.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Polny is an established edition normalization; the pinned dictionary marks the direct adjective as pȯlny or popȯlny.; The Polish and Serbian sources state the criterion deductively, while the Russian and Open Logic sources state it semantically; completeness of first-order logic makes the criteria coincide under their respective theory conventions, but the entry preserves the Open Logic semantic locator as authority.

## OLISV-T0397 · model-theory.computable-model

- **English lemma / sense:** computable model — A model presented as a computable arithmetic structure on the natural numbers; in this unit, the term is used for models of Q, PA, and true arithmetic.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izčislimy model`; lemma `izčislimy model`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Izčislimy agrees with masculine model; nominative plural is izčislimy modeli. The adjective describes the presentation of the structure, not the cardinality of its domain.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изчислимы модел`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The phrase directly combines the active computability and model heads, has a pinned Russian specialist counterpart, and stays visibly distinct from izčisljivy model, the canon's countable-model phrase.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.95 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0397-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: вычислимая модель; uk: обчислювана модель; pl: model obliczalny; cs: vyčíslitelný model; bcs: izračunljiv model; израчунљив модел; bg: изчислим модел.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[2804]; dictionary_id=5879; active canon=OLISV-T0023-r0001); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[11194]; dictionary_id=19859; active canon=OLISV-T0017-r0002); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic and standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** izčisljivy model: Canon T0384 reserves this surface for countable model; using it here would erase the theorem's computability claim.; algoritmičny model: This suggests an algorithmic presentation but is not the canon's established computable adjective and weakens exact term continuity.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** West- and South-Slavic full phrases are componential comparisons rather than direct pinned specialist quotations.

## OLISV-T0396 · model-theory.computable-structure

- **English lemma / sense:** computable structure — An arithmetic structure with domain the natural numbers whose successor, addition, and multiplication interpretations are computable functions and whose order is decidable.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izčislima struktura`; lemma `izčislima struktura`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Izčislima agrees with feminine struktura. Use izčislimu strukturu in the accusative and izčislime struktury in the nominative plural.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изчислима структура`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The compound composes two already active canon heads without creating a synonym. It preserves the source's exact distinction: the operations are computable, while the order relation is decidable.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.93 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0396-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: вычислимая структура; uk: обчислювана структура; pl: struktura obliczalna; cs: vyčíslitelná struktura; bcs: izračunljiva struktura; израчунљива структура; bg: изчислима структура.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[2804]; dictionary_id=5879; active canon=OLISV-T0023-r0001); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16426]; dictionary_id=16363; active canon=OLISV-T0016-r0001); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic and standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** izčisljiva struktura: The -ljiv- form is reserved by canon T0066/T0067 for countability or enumerability and would collapse two different properties.; kompjutabilna struktura: The internationalism is less transparent than the accepted izčisl- family and breaks continuity with canon T0023.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The full compound is an edition-level composition; the frozen official dictionary does not list the specialist phrase as one headword.

## OLISV-T0384 · model-theory.countable-model

- **English lemma / sense:** countable or enumerable model — A model whose domain is finite or countably infinite under the edition's inclusive enumerable convention; the OLP-0194 compactness theory forces the present model to be infinite.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izčisljivy model`; lemma `izčisljivy model`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine singular izčisljivy model. The adjective describes the domain's cardinality, not effective computability; use izčisljivo bezkonečny only when countable infinitude is foregrounded.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изчисљивы модел`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Izčisljivy model composes the established inclusive countability adjective with model. The use note explicitly blocks confusion with izčislimy, which denotes computability, and records why finite inclusion is harmless although the current construction is necessarily infinite.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.7 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0384-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: счётная модель; uk: зліченна модель; pl: przeliczalny model; cs: spočetný model; bcs: prebrojiv model; bg: изброим модел.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-NONSTANDARD-MODELS (lines 79-113 plus localized proof completion); OL-COM-DOWNWARD-LS (lines 13-23); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[2804]; dictionary_id=5879; lemma=izčisliti); PL-UW-MATHEMATICAL-LOGIC-2014-15 (printed page 27 / extracted lines 1358-1367); UK-KNU-MODEL-THEORY-2023 (decoded lines 1944-2008); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** izčislimy model: This suggests a computable presentation or decision procedure rather than countable cardinality.; izčisljivo bezkonečny model: Extensionally correct here but stronger in surface wording than the source's inclusive enumerable term.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The specialist cardinality adjective is derived from the established canon term rather than directly listed as such in the official dictionary.

## OLISV-T0166 · model-theory.covered-structure

- **English lemma / sense:** covered structure — A structure in which every element of the domain is the semantic value of some closed term.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `pokryta struktura`; lemma `pokryta struktura`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The resultative participle agrees with struktura: nominative singular pokryta struktura, masculine model pokryty, and non-masculine plural pokryte struktury.

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Pokryta struktura preserves the source label and states the defined extensional condition as a completed property. ## OLISV-T0181 · model-theory.definability

- **English lemma / sense:** definability — The possibility of defining one concept or relation in terms of other primitives of a theory, so it need not receive a separate interpretation in each model.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `definovateljnost`; lemma `definovateljnost`; feminine abstract noun.

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Definovateljnost keeps the directly attested defin- international root and selects a -telj- ability formation that is transparent in Polish, Czech, Slovak, and South Slavic traditions. The source explanation ties the noun to elimination of a separately interpreted primitive, making the specialist sense explicit.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** definovajemosť: The -jemosť pattern is productive and more directly analogous to a frozen dictionary row, but definovateljnost better bridges Polish definiowalność, Czech/Slovak definovatelnost, and South Slavic -ljiv/-abil- traditions while remaining transparent to East Slavic readers.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No frozen Interslavic dictionary row directly attests definovateljnost.; The selected derivation is an editorial pan-Slavic compromise among several productive national formations.

## OLISV-T0361 · model-theory.dense-linear-order-without-endpoints

- **English lemma / sense:** dense linear order without endpoints — A strict dense linear order having neither a least element nor a greatest element.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `gųsty linearny poredok bez krajnyh elementov`; lemma `gųsty linearny poredok bez krajnyh elementov`; masculine noun phrase with prepositional modifier.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Plural gųste linearne poredky bez krajnyh elementov. At first definition, gloss bez krajnyh elementov explicitly as bez najmenšego i največšego elementa.
- **Kirilična forma:** `густы линеарны поредок без крајных елементов`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The compact phrase keeps the Open Logic technical label while the mandatory first-use gloss states the exact least/greatest semantics for readers who do not know the English term. Gųsty is directly supported by the official dictionary and West/South Slavic witnesses; linearny poredok reuses OLISV-T0038; krajny is an official dictionary component whose potentially broader everyday sense is controlled by the explicit gloss and six axioms.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.9 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0361-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: плотный линейный порядок без концевых точек; uk: щільний лінійний порядок без найменшого і найбільшого елемента; pl: gęsty porządek liniowy bez elementu największego i najmniejszego; cs: husté lineární uspořádání bez nejmenšího a největšího prvku; bcs: gust linearno uređen skup bez najmanjeg i najvećeg elementa; bg: гъста линейна наредба без най-малък и най-голям елемент.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER (lines 10-26); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=5308 gųsty; dictionary_id=757 krajny); RU-MI-RAS-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2015 (physical PDF page 12, problem 10); PL-PW-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2022 (physical/printed PDF page 23, Zadanie 181); HR-ZAGREB-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2015 (physical PDF page 52, printed page 48, Theorem 1.77); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/dist/index.browser.min.js; isv-Cyrl)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** gųsty linearny poredok bez koncov: The short calque is superficially close to Russian but can be read as temporal or physical ends and does not identify the least/greatest order elements for a general reader.; gųsty linearny poredok bez granic: Boundary language is not the order-theoretic concept and risks suggesting boundedness in a metric or topological sense.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Krajny is broader in ordinary language than the specialist endpoint sense, so the explicit first-use gloss remains part of the house policy.; No official Interslavic specialist dictionary entry for the entire phrase was found in the bounded evidence set.

## OLISV-T0084 · model-theory.domain

- **English lemma / sense:** domain of a structure — The underlying set of objects over which a first-order structure interprets its language; in a many-sorted structure, also the carrier assigned to one sort before the carriers are combined in the first-order encoding.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `domen struktury`; lemma `domen struktury`; masculine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive singular domena struktury. Bare domen is allowed when context fixes the structure; use domen sorta for one sort's carrier. Do not conflate this with domen funkcije.
- **Kirilična forma:** `домен структуры`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The established domain head remains stable. OLP-0176 adds a controlled many-sorted extension: each sort has a carrier domain, while the embedding combines those carriers into one first-order domain.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.85 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0084-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: домен структуры; uk: домен структури; pl: dziedzina struktury; domena struktury; cs: doména struktury; univerzum struktury; bcs: domena strukture; bg: домейн на структурата.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[5559]; dictionary_id=36356); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** univerzum struktury: Common in some traditions but can suggest a broader set-theoretic universe.; oblast struktury: Ordinary spatial and administrative readings are less precise than the direct headword.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National accounts also use universe/carrier terminology; domen remains the edition head.

## OLISV-T0351 · model-theory.elementary-equivalence

- **English lemma / sense:** elementary equivalence — The relation between first-order structures that satisfy exactly the same sentences in the common language.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `elementarna ekvivalentnost`; lemma `elementarna ekvivalentnost`; feminine abstract noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use elementarna ekvivalentnost for the relation. In predicative and attributive use, inflect elementarno ekvivalentny for the gender, number, and case of the structures being compared.
- **Kirilična forma:** `елементарна еквивалентност`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Elementarna ekvivalentnost preserves the international elementary-equivalence term family while using two independently published Interslavic components. Direct Ukrainian, Czech, Polish, Montenegrin, and Croatian academic evidence shows that the same transparent family is recognizable across East, West, and South Slavic model theory. The adjective in the translated definition is inflected to agree with struktura, while the canon head remains the abstract name of the relation.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.96 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0351-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: элементарная эквивалентность; uk: елементарна еквівалентність; pl: równoważność elementarna; cs: elementární ekvivalence; bcs: elementarna ekvivalencija; елементарна еквиваленција; bg: елементарна еквивалентност.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ISOMORPHISM (lines 12-34 and 62-66); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6009], wordList[6276]; dictionary_id=35208,36133; elementarny and ekvivalentny); UK-KNU-ISOMORPHISM-AUTOMORPHISM-ELEMENTARY-EQUIVALENCE-2022 (physical/printed PDF page 127, section 4.4); CS-CUNI-ISOMORPHISM-ELEMENTARY-EQUIVALENCE (physical PDF pages 24-25, printed pages 96-98); PL-UW-ISOMORPHISM-AUTOMORPHISM (physical PDF pages 15-18); ME-UCG-MODEL-ISOMORPHISM (physical PDF pages 84-85); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** elementarna ravnovrednost: Ravnovrednost is broadly intelligible but can suggest equal value rather than equivalence with respect to all first-order sentences, and it weakens continuity with the attested specialist ekvivalent- family.; logična ekvivalentnost struktur: This risks confusion with logical equivalence of formulas; elementary equivalence is specifically a relation between structures satisfying the same first-order sentences.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The official Interslavic dictionary pins the two components separately, not the complete model-theory phrase.; The Ukrainian textbook phrases its definition over formulas; the Open Logic locator remains authority for the edition's sentence-only formulation.

## OLISV-T0385 · model-theory.elementary-substructure

- **English lemma / sense:** elementary substructure — A substructure that agrees with the ambient structure on the truth of every first-order formula with parameters from the substructure; used here to preserve the expanded theory and named constant while reducing cardinality.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `elementarna podstruktura`; lemma `elementarna podstruktura`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine singular elementarna podstruktura; genitive elementarnoj podstruktury. Do not weaken it to an arbitrary podstruktura, which need not preserve all formulas.
- **Kirilična forma:** `елементарна подструктура`

## OLISV-T0347 · model-theory.expansion

- **English lemma / sense:** expansion — A structure for a larger language that keeps the same domain and the same interpretations of every symbol from the smaller language while adding interpretations for the additional symbols.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `razširjenje`; lemma `råzširjeńje`; neuter noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The dictionary preserves etymological råzširjeńje; running standard-flavor prose uses razširjenje, genitive razširjenja, instrumental razširjenjem, and nominative plural razširjenja.
- **Kirilična forma:** `разширјење`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Razširjenje is the direct Interslavic dictionary head for extension and expansion after standard-flavor normalization. Its root is transparent across East, West, and South Slavic comparanda, it already appears consistently in the accepted Henkin-expansion material, and the Serbian model-theory witness uses the cognate proširenje for language expansion. It therefore gives broader practical comprehension than the competing internationalism ekspansija while preserving the exact Open Logic sense.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.98 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0347-r0001`.

- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-REDUCTS-EXPANSIONS (lines 12-39 and 59-65); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6582]; dictionary_id=3601; lemma=råzširjeńje; gloss=extension, expansion, extension, widening); OL-COM-HENKIN-ISV (lines 11, 18, 74-75, and 80-82); CS-VPL-REDUCT-EXPANSION (physical PDF page 71, printed slides 35-40); SR-KG-REDUCT-EXPANSION-2013 (physical PDF page 74, printed page 69: L* proširenje jezika L); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** ekspansija: The dictionary directly attests this international alternative, and Czech specialist usage supports it, but the native razšir- family has broader cross-Slavic convergence and already governs the accepted edition's logical expansion prose.; proširjenje: This South-Slavic-weighted prefix is directly understandable and domain-attested, but razširjenje better aligns the broader East/West/South dictionary convergence and the edition's existing canonical surface.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The dictionary head covers extension, expansion, and widening; the Open Logic locator supplies the specialist model-theory sense boundary.; Czech technical usage favors the competing international expanze, while South-Slavic usage prefers proširenje; the selected razširjenje is an editorial pan-Slavic compromise.

- **English lemma / sense:** extension — A structure for the same language whose domain includes that of a substructure and whose interpretations restrict to the substructure interpretations on the smaller domain.

Reusing it here reflects broad East-West-South convergence, while the separate canon key prevents the same-language larger-domain relation from being confused with OLP-0184's richer-language, same-domain expansion. The Polish evidence combines a direct podstruktura definition with the dictionary's cognate rozszerzenie family; the Serbian source exposes ekstenzija as a real same-neighborhood specialist alternative, but the native family remains more broadly transparent.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.97 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0349-r0001`.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** ekstenzija: The Serbian source directly attests this internationalism for a structural extension, but the native razšir- family is directly present in the Interslavic dictionary and converges more broadly across the comparison languages.; nadstruktura: This would be a transparent reverse compound, but it would create an unnecessary new head where the dictionary already supplies extension and the Open Logic source explicitly labels the reverse relation as extension.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** zamysljena struktura: The phrase is readily understood in several Slavic languages, but the frozen positive adjective zamysljeny is glossed primarily as pensive or thoughtful; naměrjena better isolates the intended-as-designed sense needed here.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No frozen row directly attests the positive full specialist phrase naměrjena struktura.; National model-theory traditions vary between intended, presumed, envisaged, and designated semantic nuances.

## OLISV-T0400 · model-theory.interpolation-theorem

- **English lemma / sense:** interpolation theorem — Craig's interpolation theorem: if a sentence entails another, an interpolant can be expressed using only their common nonlogical vocabulary.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `teorema interpolacije`; lemma `teorema interpolacije`; feminine noun with genitive complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Teorema inflects as a feminine noun; interpolacije remains the genitive singular complement. Craigova teorema interpolacije is the explicit named variant used when attribution must be visible.
- **Kirilična forma:** `теорема интерполације`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Teorema interpolacije transparently combines the established canon head teorema with the internationally shared interpolation stem. Russian, Polish, Czech, and Croatian academic sources independently preserve that stem while varying between genitive, prepositional, adjectival, and possessive constructions. The genitive complement is compact, pan-Slavically legible, and matches the source's unattributed chapter title; Craigova teorema interpolacije remains the explicit named variant.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.92 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0400-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: теорема Крейга об интерполяции; uk: теорема Крейга про інтерполяцію; pl: Twierdzenie Craiga o interpolacji; cs: Craigova věta o interpolaci; bcs: Craigova interpolacija; Teorem (Craig); bg: теорема за интерполация.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-INTUITIONISTIC-LOGIC (active canon=OLISV-T0273-r0001); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** interpolacijska teorema: Readable, but the adjective is less directly aligned with the strong cross-Slavic genitive/prepositional evidence and can suggest a broader theorem class.; teorema o interpolaciji: Close to Russian and Czech/Polish prepositional patterns, but longer than the equally transparent genitive construction selected for the chapter heading.; Craigova teorema interpolacije: Mathematically exact and retained as the explicit named variant, but the Open Logic chapter title deliberately omits Craig's name.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source title is generic although the chapter develops Craig's theorem; the named variant is recorded for downstream occurrences.; No official Interslavic dictionary head for interpolacija is pinned; the selected internationalism is supported by four academic Slavic traditions and remains reversible.

## OLISV-T0365 · model-theory.models-of-arithmetic

- **English lemma / sense:** models of arithmetic — Structures that interpret an arithmetic language or satisfy an arithmetic theory, including standard, non-standard, Q, PA, and computable models in the chapter scope.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `modeli aritmetiky`; lemma `modeli aritmetiky`; masculine plural noun phrase with feminine genitive-singular complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Singular model aritmetiky; plural modeli aritmetiky; genitive plural modelov aritmetiky. Retain aritmetiky as the complement rather than turning it into an adjective.
- **Kirilična forma:** `модели аритметикы`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Modeli aritmetiky is the shortest transparent Interslavic rendering of the Open Logic chapter head. It composes the established canon head model (OLISV-T0017) with the directly attested aritmetika component already used in OLISV-T0187 and OLISV-T0205. The genitive complement matches Russian, Ukrainian, Polish, Czech, and Bosnian/Croatian/Serbian structure, while Bulgarian independently confirms the same semantic relation analytically. The phrase preserves the distinction between a plurality of structures and arithmetic as either language or theory.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.97 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0365-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: модели арифметики; uk: моделі арифметики; pl: modele arytmetyki; cs: modely aritmetiky; bcs: modeli aritmetike; модели аритметике; bg: модели на аритметиката.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-CHAPTER-DRIVER (line 8 and ordered imports on lines 10-20); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[11194]; dictionary_id=19859; lemma=model; gloss=model); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1093]; dictionary_id=21256; lemma=aritmetika; gloss=arithmetics); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/dist/index.browser.min.js; isv-Cyrl)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** aritmetične modeli: The adjective can suggest models used for calculation or models having merely arithmetic properties, while the genitive construction more clearly preserves the source relation and dominates the comparison traditions.; modeli aritmetičnoj teorije: This is too narrow because the chapter includes structures, languages, standard and non-standard models, Q, PA, and computability rather than one single arithmetic theory.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete specialist phrase is a transparent project-house composition of directly attested official Interslavic components rather than a standalone official dictionary headword.; Arithmetic is intentionally left broad between language and theory at chapter level; child units will narrow the sense where Open Logic does.

## OLISV-T0358 · model-theory.n-elementary-equivalence

- **English lemma / sense:** n-equivalence — Agreement of two structures on the truth of every sentence whose quantifier rank is at most n.

- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine ekvivalentnost; structures are n-elementarno ekvivalentne. Retain the index n as a mathematical metavariable in typeset prose even though the deterministic plain-text Cyrillic head projects its letter.
- **Kirilična forma:** `н-елементарна еквивалентност`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** N-elementarna ekvivalentnost explicitly connects the bounded notion to OLISV-T0351 elementarna ekvivalentnost and matches the direct Polish and Croatian indexed terms. The qualifier prevents confusion with game n-equivalence, which the Russian syllabus explicitly distinguishes. The source's shorter English n-equivalent label remains searchable in the English field and definition.

- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: формульная n-эквивалентность; модели n-эквивалентны; uk: n-елементарна еквівалентність; pl: m-elementarna równoważność; cs: n-elementární ekvivalence; bcs: n-elementarna ekvivalentnost; bg: n-елементарна еквивалентност.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM (lines 116-124 and 248-261); RU-HSE-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE-2018 (physical PDF pages 2-3, items 45-49); PL-UW-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical/printed PDF page 4, Definition 13); HR-ZAGREB-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical PDF page 124, printed page 116); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=36133; ekvivalentny; plus established OLISV-T0351 elementary-equivalence components); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** n-ekvivalentnost: This literal rendering is short and directly resembles Russian usage, but without elementarna it can be confused with game, formula, algebraic, or other indexed equivalences.; formulna n-ekvivalentnost: The Russian qualifier is precise, but the Open Logic definition applies to structures via sentences, while Polish and Croatian directly anchor the notion in elementary equivalence.
