## OLISV-T0380 · model-theory.nonstandard-arithmetic-structure

- **English lemma / sense:** non-standard arithmetic structure — A structure for the first-order language of arithmetic that is not isomorphic to the distinguished natural-number structure, whether or not it satisfies a specified arithmetic theory.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `nestandardna struktura za jezyk aritmetiky`; lemma `nestandardna struktura za jezyk aritmetiky`; feminine noun phrase with prepositional complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use short nestandardna struktura when the arithmetic-language context is explicit. Reserve nestandardny model aritmetiky for a structure known to satisfy the relevant arithmetic theory.
- **Kirilična forma:** `нестандардна структура за језык аритметикы`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The explicit language complement prevents model from being used before satisfaction has been established. It also makes the source's integer-domain counterexample readable without falsely calling that structure a model of true arithmetic.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nestandardny model aritmetiky: This stronger term asserts modelhood and is false of the source's integer-domain example.; nestandardna aritmetika: This can denote a discipline or theory rather than a structure interpreting the arithmetic language.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The full phrase is a transparent edition compound rather than a standalone official dictionary headword.

## OLISV-T0350 · model-theory.overspill-compactness-corollary

- **English lemma / sense:** overspill (compactness corollary) — The compactness consequence that a set of first-order sentences with arbitrarily large finite models also has an infinite model; here this is not the nonstandard-analysis overspill principle and not computing overflow.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `prěhod k bezkonečnosti`; lemma `prěhod k bezkonečnosti`; masculine noun phrase with dative complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use the fixed section-title form prěhod k bezkonečnosti. Outside the title, inflect the head regularly (for example, genitive prěhoda) while keeping k plus dative bezkonečnosti.
- **Kirilična forma:** `прєход к безконечности`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The surveyed Ukrainian and Czech academic treatments present the result as a theorem or example derived from compactness, while the Polish and Croatian sources independently confirm the key finite-model and countably-many-constants wording. No stable cross-Slavic specialist calque of the source label was found in the bounded survey. Prěhod k bezkonečnosti therefore names the exact mathematical movement in transparent, dictionary-attested components and remains immediately parsable to Slavic-language readers without importing the misleading computing-overflow or nonstandard-analysis senses of overspill.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.86 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0350-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: переход к бесконечности; uk: перехід до нескінченності; pl: przejście do nieskończoności; cs: přechod k nekonečnu; bcs: prijelaz/prelaz ka beskonačnosti; пријелаз/прелаз ка бесконачности; bg: преход към безкрайността.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-OVERSPILL (lines 11-42); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[17556]; dictionary_id=19506; lemma=prěhod; gloss=transition); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[9220]; dictionary_id=6519; lemma=bezkonėčnosť; gloss=infinity, perpetuality); UK-KNU-COMPACTNESS-INFINITE-MODEL-2023 (physical PDF page 95, printed page 94, Theorem 4.20); CS-CUNI-COMPACTNESS-INFINITE-MODEL (physical PDF page 32, printed page 31); PL-UW-ARBITRARILY-LARGE-FINITE-MODELS (physical PDF page 8, problem 12(b,d)); HR-ZAGREB-COUNTABLY-MANY-CONSTANTS (physical PDF page 25, printed page 19); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** prěpolnjenje: A literal spill/overflow calque would invite computing-overflow and unrelated logical-explosion senses, while the checked academic theorem sources do not use such a title.; overspill: Retaining the unexplained English label would be opaque to readers who do not know English and would collide with the better-known nonstandard-analysis principle; the source label remains recoverable from the English witness and canon key.; poslědstvo kompaktnosti: bezkonečny model: This is semantically exact and remains a documented explanatory alternative, but it reads as a synopsis rather than a concise section name and repeats terminology made explicit in the first theorem.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete Interslavic title is a transparent editorial phrase, not a frozen dictionary headword or directly attested national specialist title.; The bounded survey supports absence of a stable cross-Slavic Overspill calque in the checked sources but cannot prove universal absence across all Slavic scholarship.; Prěhod k bezkonečnosti can be read more broadly outside this section; the canon key and theorem locator fix the compactness-corollary sense.

## OLISV-T0355 · model-theory.partial-isomorphism

- **English lemma / sense:** partial isomorphism — A finite partial function between two structures that satisfies the isomorphism conditions wherever the function is defined.

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Čestičny izomorfizm reuses the canon's established native partial adjective from OLISV-T0037 and the structure-isomorphism head from OLISV-T0352. Russian and Polish directly support the same native part-based family; Croatian supplies a directly attested international alternative. The definition, not superficial word similarity, fixes the finite partial-function sense.

## OLISV-T0359 · model-theory.purely-relational-language

- **English lemma / sense:** purely relational language — A first-order language whose nonlogical vocabulary contains relation or predicate symbols but no function symbols, with the treatment of constants stated locally by the source.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `čisto relacijsky jezyk`; lemma `čisto relacijsky jezyk`; masculine adverb-adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine phrase; genitive čisto relacijskogo jezyka. The accompanying definition must state whether constants are excluded, because the source itself changes that convention between passages.
- **Kirilična forma:** `чисто релацијскы језык`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Čisto relacijsky jezyk is a transparent compositional label: čisto excludes nonrelational operations, relacijsky identifies the signature family, and jezyk remains the edition's ordinary language head. The entry records the source's deliberate local convention shift—one theorem excludes constants while a later finite-language proposition permits them—so the head is never used as a substitute for checking the adjacent definition.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0359-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: чисто реляционный язык; uk: суто реляційна мова; pl: czysto relacyjny język; cs: čistě relační jazyk; bcs: čisto relacijski jezik; bg: чисто релационен език.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM (lines 83-108 and 126-138); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=582 čisty; 541 język; established relation-family components); PL-UW-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical/printed PDF pages 2 and 4); HR-ZAGREB-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical PDF pages 20-21); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** bezfunkcijny jezyk: This describes the most important negative condition but hides the positive relational vocabulary and still leaves the status of constants ambiguous.; predikatny jezyk: Predicate language is recognizable but can be misread as the whole first-order language family and does not foreground the no-function restriction.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The source itself excludes constants in its first purely relational theorem but permits constants in its later finite-language proposition.; The full phrase is a compositional edition choice; only its relational components are directly attested in the pinned Polish and Croatian sources.

## OLISV-T0357 · model-theory.quantifier-rank

- **English lemma / sense:** quantifier rank — The maximum nesting depth of quantifiers in a formula, recursively defined as a natural number.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `kvantifikatorny rang`; lemma `kvantifikatorny rang`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine inanimate phrase; genitive kvantifikatornogo ranga and instrumental kvantifikatornym rangom. Rank measures nesting depth, not social or matrix rank.
- **Kirilična forma:** `квантификаторны ранг`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Kvantifikatorny rang follows the convergent adjective-plus-rank structure directly attested in Russian, Polish, and Croatian while reusing the edition's established kvantifikator head. The adjectival form is more faithful to all three academic witnesses than an unexplained genitive paraphrase, and the definition explicitly limits the sense to quantifier nesting depth.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.97 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0357-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: кванторный ранг; uk: кванторний ранг; pl: ranga kwantyfikatorowa; cs: kvantifikátorový rang; bcs: kvantifikatorski rang; bg: кванторен ранг.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM (lines 112-124); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=16072; rang; gloss level; and OLISV-T0100's pinned kvantifikator source components); RU-HSE-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE-2018 (physical PDF page 2, item 45); PL-UW-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical/printed PDF page 3, Definition 10); HR-ZAGREB-PARTIAL-ISOMORPHISM-QUANTIFIER-RANK-N-EQUIVALENCE (physical PDF page 21, Definition 1.24); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** rang kvantifikatorov: The genitive phrase is transparent, but every pinned direct national witness uses an adjective and the edition already forms kvantifikatorny regularly.; glubina kvantifikatorov: Depth is explanatory but loses direct correspondence with the rank notation and the strongly convergent Slavic academic rank family.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Russian commonly shortens quantifier to квантор in the compound, whereas the edition retains the already canonical full head kvantifikator.; Some Czech sources may prefer a depth term rather than the international rank family.

## OLISV-T0364 · model-theory.rational-order

- **English lemma / sense:** rational order — The linear order carried by the rational numbers, considered as an order structure or order type rather than as a field.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `poredok racionaľnyh čisl`; lemma `poredok racionaľnyh čisl`; masculine noun phrase with genitive plural complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use poredok racionaľnyh čisl or poredkovy tip struktury Q; avoid bare racionaľny poredok because it can be read as a reasonable order.
- **Kirilična forma:** `поредок рационалных числ`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The genitive construction names the mathematical carrier explicitly and reuses OLISV-T0253 for racionaľno čislo plus OLISV-T0038 for poredok. Polish and Croatian witnesses both present Q as the canonical order structure. This head prevents the adjective racionaľny from being misread in its ordinary sense of reasonable and prevents the order reduct from being confused with the rational field.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.89 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0364-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: порядок рациональных чисел; uk: порядок раціональних чисел; pl: porządek (Q, <=); cs: uspořádání racionálních čísel; bcs: uređaj skupa Q; bg: наредбата на рационалните числа.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER (lines 69-83); PL-PW-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2022 (physical/printed PDF page 23, Zadanie 181); HR-ZAGREB-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2015 (physical PDF page 52, printed page 48, Theorem 1.77); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/dist/index.browser.min.js; isv-Cyrl)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** racionaľny poredok: The adjective can be read as reasonable or rationally chosen and does not state that the carrier is the set of rational numbers.; racionaľna struktura: This can denote the field or another structure on the rationals and therefore loses the order-reduct boundary.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The selected Interslavic head is a transparent project-house compound rather than a directly attested official specialist dictionary entry.; Order-type notation eta may be added in a later unit if Open Logic introduces it explicitly; it is not silently imported here.

## OLISV-T0346 · model-theory.reduct

- **English lemma / sense:** reduct — The structure obtained from a structure in a larger language by retaining the same domain and forgetting only the interpretations of symbols outside the smaller language.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `redukt`; lemma `redukt`; masculine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use nominative redukt, genitive redukta, instrumental reduktom, and nominative plural redukty. Reserve redukcija for the operation or process rather than the resulting structure.
- **Kirilična forma:** `редукт`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Redukt is the transparent result noun required by the Open Logic definition. Czech teaching material explicitly distinguishes redukce from the resulting redukt, and the Serbian model-theory witness uses redukt in the same restriction-to-a-smaller-language construction. The frozen dictionary confirms the reduk- family across all comparison regions. This is therefore more precise than using redukcija for both process and object.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.89 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0346-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: редукция; uk: редукція; pl: redukcja; cs: redukt; redukce; bcs: redukt; redukcija; редукт; редукција; bg: редукция.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-REDUCTS-EXPANSIONS (lines 24-39; definition defn:reduct); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14072]; dictionary_id=30601; lemma=redukcija; gloss=reduction); CS-VPL-REDUCT-EXPANSION (physical PDF page 71, printed slides 35-40: redukce struktury; redukt M = M'|L); SR-KG-REDUCT-EXPANSION-2013 (physical PDF page 74, printed page 69: Ako je A redukt od A* na L); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** redukcija struktury: The dictionary-attested noun primarily names the reduction process; using it for the resulting structure would erase the process-versus-object distinction explicitly visible in Czech specialist usage.; ograničena struktura: A restricted-structure paraphrase is understandable but risks confusion with restriction, substructure, or boundedness and lacks the concise specialist symmetry with razširjenje.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The pinned Interslavic dictionary contains redukcija but no direct redukt headword; redukt is therefore a specialist editorial formation rather than a claimed general-standard lemma.; Direct exact-sense evidence is strongest in Czech and Serbian; other comparison columns currently establish the root family rather than the complete model-theory term.

## OLISV-T0202 · model-theory.relation-expression

- **English lemma / sense:** formula expressing a relation — The satisfaction-relative condition in which a formula with specified free variables holds under exactly those assignments whose values stand in a given relation.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izražanje odnošenja formulom`; lemma `izražanje odnošenja formulom`; neuter verbal noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter head izražanje with genitive odnošenja and instrumental formulom; in clauses use formula izražaje odnošenje.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изражање одношења формулом`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The phrase preserves the source's distinction between a formula expressing a relation and the broader model-theoretic concept of definability. All three lexical components are directly registered and the controlled clause states the formal operation naturally.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.91 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0202-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: выражение отношения формулой; uk: вираження відношення формулою; pl: wyrażanie relacji formułą; cs: vyjádření relace formulí; bcs: izražavanje relacije formulom; изражавање релације формулом; bg: изразяване на отношение с формула.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6564]; dictionary_id=682; lemma=izražati; gloss=express); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[7255]; dictionary_id=5181; lemma=formula; gloss=formula); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14174]; dictionary_id=1992; lemma=odnošeńje; gloss=relation); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** definovanje odnošenja: This collapses expression into the broader definability concept and obscures the assignment-relative satisfaction condition that the source defines first.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete verbal-noun phrase is editorial packaging; the controlled clause formula izražaje odnošenje is the primary running-prose form.

## OLISV-T0063 · model-theory.saturated-set

- **English lemma / sense:** saturated set — A formula set containing a constant witness for each existential formula in the set.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `nasyćeno množstvo`; lemma `nasyćeno množstvo`; neuter noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The past passive participle nasyćeny agrees with množstvo; use neuter singular nasyćeno množstvo and abstract noun nasyćenost.
- **Kirilična forma:** `насычено множство`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Nasyćeno množstvo preserves the widely recognizable Slavic saturation root, uses the official participial formation, and directly mirrors the defined property of having enough witnesses; the technical definition prevents the everyday satiation sense from carrying the analysis.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.8 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0063-r0001`.

- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14814-14816]; dictionary_id=28260,28262,28261); ISV-GRAMMAR-VERBS (lines 180-182); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard flavorization ć→č before StandardCyrillic type=5)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** saturirano množstvo: The international loan is recognizable to specialists but is less directly parsable across the target readership than the strongly attested native saturation family.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The dictionary attests the lexical family rather than the exact model-theoretic compound; the phrase remains an explicitly documented edition-level composition.

## OLISV-T0171 · model-theory.skolem-theorem

- **English lemma / sense:** Skolem's theorem — The first-order Skolemization result used here: a formula of the form for every x there exists y A(x,y) is satisfiable exactly when an expansion with a fresh function symbol satisfies for every x A(x,f(x)).
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `teorema Skolema`; lemma `teorema Skolema`; feminine proper-name noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive teoremy Skolema. Keep the genitive surname construction aligned with teorema Lovenheima-Skolema (OLISV-T0088); do not conflate this specific Skolemization result with that different named theorem.
- **Kirilična forma:** `теорема Сколема`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The genitive surname construction is already established by OLISV-T0088 and is directly intelligible across the comparison traditions; the separate canon ID prevents accidental identification of Skolemization with the Löwenheim–Skolem theorem.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.87 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0171-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: теорема Сколема; uk: теорема Сколема; pl: twierdzenie Skolema; cs: Skolemova věta; bcs: Skolemov teorem; Сколемова теорема; bg: теорема на Сколем.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** Skolemova teorema: The possessive adjective is grammatical and common in several traditions, but the genitive construction matches the edition's existing named-theorem policy and reduces competing surname paradigms.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The label Skolem's theorem is used for several related results in the literature; the canon sense is restricted to the exact statement in the pinned Open Logic unit.; No frozen dictionary row directly attests the proper-name phrase.

## OLISV-T0205 · model-theory.standard-arithmetic-model

- **English lemma / sense:** standard model of arithmetic — The natural-number structure N with the ordinary interpretations of the arithmetic vocabulary, or any L_A-structure isomorphic to it. Standardness is therefore invariant under isomorphism and does not assert that another model's domain elements are literally numbers.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `standardny model aritmetiky`; lemma `standardny model aritmetiky`; masculine noun phrase with genitive complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine phrase; genitive standardnogo modela aritmetiky and locative v standardnom modelu aritmetiky.
- **Kirilična forma:** `стандардны модел аритметикы`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The surface remains unchanged because its three components are transparent and strongly convergent. Revision 2 repairs the sense boundary: this unit defines every structure isomorphic to N as standard, so the canon may not restrict the head to N alone or identify an isomorphic domain's objects literally with natural numbers.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.98 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0205-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: стандартная модель арифметики; uk: стандартна модель арифметики; pl: standardowy model arytmetyki; cs: standardní model aritmetiky; bcs: standardni model aritmetike; стандардни модел аритметике; bg: стандартен модел на аритметиката.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-STANDARD-MODELS (lines 12-23 and 43-50); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16187,11194,1093]; dictionary_id=20754,19859,21256); RU-MI-RAS-PROVABILITY-ARITHMETIC-2020 (physical page 1 / extracted lines 26-28); UK-KNU-MODEL-THEORY-2023 (physical page 37 / decoded extract lines 1810-1815); CS-CUNI-LOGIC-II-2008 (physical page 1 / extracted lines 23-30); HR-ZAGREB-GODEL-INCOMPLETENESS-2011 (printed page 4 / physical page 6 / extracted lines 172-193); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** standardny model označaje jedino Struct N: This is the previous sense's over-narrow implication. OLP-0193 explicitly includes every structure isomorphic to N.; obyčny model aritmetiky: Ordinary suggests typicality and loses the conventional model-theoretic reference class fixed by isomorphism.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The phrase is an edition-wide provisional technical head, not a claim of official Interslavic or native-speaker certification.; A standard model may have a domain whose elements are not literally natural numbers; only the arithmetic structure up to isomorphism is asserted.

## OLISV-T0394 · model-theory.standard-part

- **English lemma / sense:** standard part of a model — The elements corresponding to the standard natural numbers inside a model of arithmetic.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `standardna čest modela`; lemma `standardna čest modela`; feminine adjective-noun phrase with genitive complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Standardna agrees with feminine čest; modela is genitive singular. The edition's basic reader spelling čest is retained instead of etymological čęsť.
- **Kirilična forma:** `стандардна чест модела`

## OLISV-T0353 · model-theory.structure-automorphism

- **English lemma / sense:** automorphism of a structure — An isomorphism from a first-order structure onto itself; such maps preserve every definable subset.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `avtomorfizm struktury`; lemma `avtomorfizm struktury`; masculine noun with genitive singular complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use avtomorfizm struktury for the full term and avtomorfizm when the structure is already explicit. Inflect the head regularly; use neizměnny pri avtomorfizmah for invariant under automorphisms.
- **Kirilična forma:** `автоморфизм структуры`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Avtomorfizm retains the shared specialist morph- family while adapting the prefix to standard Interslavic avto-. The same self-isomorphism concept is directly attested in Ukrainian, Polish, Czech, and Croatian academic sources, with several also connecting automorphisms to definability or invariance. The full head avtomorfizm struktury prevents ambiguity outside model theory; the unit may use the short form once the structure is explicit.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.95 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0353-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: автоморфизм структуры; uk: автоморфізм структури; pl: automorfizm struktury; cs: automorfismus struktury; bcs: automorfizam strukture; аутоморфизам структуре; bg: автоморфизъм на структура.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ISOMORPHISM (lines 122-131); UK-KNU-ISOMORPHISM-AUTOMORPHISM-ELEMENTARY-EQUIVALENCE-2022 (physical/printed PDF page 124 and pages 127-128); PL-UW-ISOMORPHISM-AUTOMORPHISM (physical PDF pages 12 and 15-18); CS-CUNI-GEOMETRIC-MODEL-THEORY-SYLLABUS (HTML lines 88-92 and 194-259); HR-ZAGREB-COUNTABLY-MANY-CONSTANTS (additional extract, physical PDF pages 17-18); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[9524], wordList[17894]; dictionary_id=19886,36418; neizměnny and neizměnjeny); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** automorfizm struktury: The unadapted international prefix auto- is recognizable but conflicts with the established Interslavic avto- adaptation used for the selected head.; samoizomorfizm struktury: This is descriptively transparent but is not the cross-Slavic specialist head attested by the academic sources and would needlessly separate the edition from standard mathematical usage.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No direct official Interslavic dictionary headword for automorphism was found in the pinned response.; The avto- prefix is an edition-level Interslavic normalization from a stable international Slavic family, not a native-language certification.

## OLISV-T0352 · model-theory.structure-isomorphism

- **English lemma / sense:** isomorphism of structures — A bijection between two first-order structures that preserves and reflects constants, predicates, and functions of their common language.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izomorfizm struktur`; lemma `izomorfizm struktur`; masculine noun with genitive plural complement.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use izomorfizm for the witnessing map and izomorfne struktury for the relation between structures; inflect izomorfizm regularly while the complement marks the compared domain.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изоморфизм структур`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Izomorfizm is the maximally shared international specialist head across the pinned Ukrainian, Polish, Czech, Montenegrin, and Croatian academic sources. The Interslavic ending -izm matches the already accepted canon family and avoids choosing a single national -ismus or -izam ending. Adding struktur disambiguates the model-theory sense from the already recorded Curry–Howard isomorphism while the running prose may use the shorter head where the domain is explicit.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.97 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0352-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: изоморфизм структур; uk: ізоморфізм структур; pl: izomorfizm struktur; cs: izomorfismus struktur; bcs: izomorfizam struktura; изоморфизам структура; bg: изоморфизъм на структури.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ISOMORPHISM (lines 36-60); UK-KNU-ISOMORPHISM-AUTOMORPHISM-ELEMENTARY-EQUIVALENCE-2022 (physical/printed PDF pages 123-124); CS-CUNI-ISOMORPHISM-ELEMENTARY-EQUIVALENCE (physical PDF pages 24-25, printed pages 96-98); PL-UW-ISOMORPHISM-AUTOMORPHISM (physical PDF page 12 and pages 15-18); HR-ZAGREB-COUNTABLY-MANY-CONSTANTS (additional extract, physical PDF pages 7-9); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; Standard Cyrillic)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** strukturna jednakost: Isomorphism is not literal equality: it permits different underlying objects connected by a structure-preserving bijection.; izomorfnost struktur: The property noun is usable, but the source immediately defines and repeatedly reasons with the witnessing map; izomorfizm keeps the map and relation terminology aligned.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No direct official Interslavic dictionary headword for isomorphism was found in the pinned response.; Russian and Bulgarian surfaces are editorial comparanda here; the edition preference rests on the directly pinned Ukrainian, West-Slavic, and South-Slavic academic family.

## OLISV-T0348 · model-theory.substructure

- **English lemma / sense:** substructure — A structure for the same language whose domain is included in a larger structure and whose constants, functions, and predicates agree with the larger structure on that included domain.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `podstruktura`; lemma `podstruktura`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Use nominative podstruktura, genitive podstruktury, instrumental podstrukturoju, and nominative plural podstruktury. Keep it distinct from podmnožstvo and from redukt.
- **Kirilična forma:** `подструктура`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Podstruktura exactly preserves the formal direction of the Open Logic relation and is immediately recognizable across East, West, and South Slavic terminology. Polish provides an almost line-for-line same-signature definition; Czech, Russian, Ukrainian, and Serbian independently attest the same compound in specialist contexts. The shared Interslavic pod and struktura components make the form morphologically transparent while the formal source keeps it distinct from an arbitrary subset and from a reduct.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.99 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0348-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: подструктура; uk: підструктура; pl: podstruktura; cs: podstruktura; bcs: podstruktura; подструктура; bg: подструктура.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-SUBSTRUCTURES (lines 12-44, especially definition defn:substructure); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16426], wordList[17919]; dictionary_id=16363,737; struktura and pod); PL-MIMUW-SUBSTRUCTURES (physical PDF page 4, Definition 16); CS-CUNI-SUBSTRUCTURES (physical PDF page 7, slide 7/15); UK-KPI-ALGEBRAIC-SUBSTRUCTURE (HTML lines 497-499); RU-HSE-MODEL-SUBSTRUCTURE (physical PDF page 2, syllabus items 23 and 34); SR-KG-REDUCT-EXPANSION-2013 (physical PDF page 27, printed page 23); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** substruktura: The Latin international prefix is interpretable, but the directly attested national-language evidence converges more strongly on pod-/pid- and the native prefix is immediately transparent across the target readership.; podmodel: A structure becomes a model only relative to a theory, while the Open Logic definition applies to arbitrary structures for one language; using model would narrow the denotation.; čest struktury: This explanatory phrase is too broad: the source explicitly warns that domain inclusion alone does not make one structure a formal part of another.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete compound is not a frozen Interslavic dictionary headword; it is a high-confidence specialist formation from two directly attested components.; The Ukrainian witness is from algebraic structures rather than model theory, while Polish, Czech, Russian, and Serbian supply the closer formal domain.

## OLISV-T0399 · model-theory.tennenbaum-theorem

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Tennenbaumova teorema combines the active theorem head with the edition's established possessive-adjective name pattern and a direct Slovak specialist occurrence. Russian and Polish directly attest the competing genitive pattern, which remains documented as a readable alternative. The definition includes the essential up-to-isomorphism qualification absent from the source sentence.

- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-INTUITIONISTIC-LOGIC (lines 42-47 and 117-126; active canon=OLISV-T0273-r0001); ISV-GRAMMAR-ADJ (lines 43-45; active named-person precedent=OLISV-T0024-r0001); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic and standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** teorema Tennenbauma: This direct Russian/Polish-style genitive construction is intelligible and evidence-backed, but the possessive adjective better matches the active Interslavic named-person pattern and direct Slovak usage.; Tennenbaumovo tvrđenje: The generic statement head obscures the established theorem category and weakens continuity with canon T0273.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** East- and West-Slavic sources divide between genitive and possessive-adjective name constructions; the chosen head is a reversible house preference.; No direct South-Slavic Tennenbaum-theorem occurrence is pinned; South-Slavic comparanda are explicitly componential.

## OLISV-T0179 · proof.axiom-independence

- **English lemma / sense:** independence of axioms — The property that no axiom in a system is semantically entailed by the remaining axioms; non-entailment may be witnessed by a model of the others plus its negation.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `nezavisnost aksiom`; lemma `nezavisnost aksiom`; feminine noun phrase.

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Nezavisnost aksiom combines two directly frozen and broadly recognizable components, while Open Logic supplies the exact model-theoretic witness condition. It also avoids confusing independence with the stronger political sense sometimes carried by nezaležnost.

- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: независимость аксиом; uk: незалежність аксіом; pl: niezależność aksjomatów; cs: nezávislost axiomů; bcs: nezavisnost aksioma; bg: независимост на аксиомите.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[9140,9143]; dictionary_id=17957,17958; lemma=nezavisnosť,nezavisny; gloss=independence,independent); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1498]; dictionary_id=24180; lemma=aksioma; gloss=axiom); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nezaležnost aksiom: Nezaležnost is a listed synonym and is strong in Ukrainian and Polish, but nezavisnost aligns directly with Russian, Czech/Slovak cognates, BCS, Macedonian, and Bulgarian while avoiding political-independence overtones.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete technical compound is assembled editorially even though both components are directly attested.

## OLISV-T0176 · proof.contraposition

- **English lemma / sense:** contraposition — The transformation or proof method that replaces an implication from A to B by the equivalent implication from not-B to not-A; in the source it proves the reverse direction through its contrapositive.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `kontrapozicija`; lemma `kontrapozicija`; feminine abstract noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Instrumental kontrapozicijoju; genitive kontrapozicije. Use for the implication transformation or proof method, not for dokaz od protivnogo and not for the converse implication.
- **Kirilična forma:** `контрапозиција`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Kontrapozicija preserves the highly convergent international logical term across East, West, and South Slavic comparison languages. Its dedicated sense note distinguishes the implication transformation from both the converse and proof by contradiction, a distinction made locally by the two adjacent proof strategies in Open Logic.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.9 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0176-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: контрапозиция; uk: контрапозиція; pl: kontrapozycja; cs: kontrapozice; bcs: kontrapozicija; контрапозиција; bg: контрапозиция.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[13187]; dictionary_id=1225; lemma=pozicija; gloss=position); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[4132]; dictionary_id=19795; lemma=kontrakcija; gloss=contraction); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[4136]; dictionary_id=4388; lemma=protivny; gloss=contrary); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** dokaz od protivnogo: Proof by contradiction assumes the negation of the desired result and derives inconsistency; it is explicitly a different method in the following proof and cannot replace contraposition.; obratno tvrđenje: The converse swaps antecedent and consequent without negating them and is not logically equivalent in general; it names a different transformation.; kontrapozitiv: This can name the resulting contrapositive conditional, whereas the source sentence and selected head denote the transformation or proof method.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No frozen Interslavic dictionary row directly attests the complete formal-logic lemma.; Some pedagogical traditions use the related noun for the resulting conditional rather than the transformation; the canon sense is explicitly method-first.

## OLISV-T0015 · proof.deduction

- **English lemma / sense:** deduction — A rule-governed inference process, especially the natural-deduction proof system.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `dedukcija`; lemma `dedukcija`; feminine noun.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** izvod: Izvod is directly listed for conclusion, deduction, and inference, but it denotes an inference result more readily than the named proof system.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No specific uncertainty beyond the provisional edition boundary.

## OLISV-T0324 · proof.defeasible-reasoning

- **English lemma / sense:** defeasible reasoning — Reasoning whose apparently reasonable conclusions may later be withdrawn when relevant new information is supplied.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `odzyvatelno razsuđanje`; lemma `odzyvatelno razsuđanje`; neuter adjective-verbal-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter razsuđanje; genitive odzyvateljnogo razsuđanja and contextual genitive plural odzyvatelnyh form razsuđanja.
- **Kirilična forma:** `одзывателно разсуджање`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Odzyvatelno razsuđanje makes reversibility under new information explicit by combining the withdrawal family with the edition's established razsuđanje head. It avoids importing the misleading everyday sense of weak or easily defeated thought.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.78 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0324-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: отменяемое рассуждение; uk: спростовне міркування; pl: rozumowanie podważalne; cs: zrušitelné usuzování; bcs: opozivo rasuđivanje; опозиво расуђивање; bg: отменимо разсъждение.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-OTHER-LOGICS (lines 25-28); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=32460; lemma=odzyvati; gloss=withdraw, recall); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=34021; lemma=råzsųđati; gloss=reason); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** odrěčimo razsuđanje: Odrěkti can suggest verbally denying a claim; the source requires the technical ability to withdraw an inference after information changes.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete adjective-noun compound is editorial, and defeasible has no single uniformly adopted Slavic calque.

## OLISV-T0077 · proof.derivation

- **English lemma / sense:** derivation — A finite formal object licensed by a proof system and establishing a derivability claim.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izvodženje`; lemma `izvodženje`; neuter verbal noun.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** dedukcija: Deduction is a broader method and not specifically the finite derivation object.; dokaz: Proof or evidence is broader than the licensed construction described here.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The direct dictionary row uses an etymological/extended surface and is polysemous across derivation, origin, and morphology.; East-Slavic readers may expect a vyvod surface.

## OLISV-T0078 · proof.derivation-system

## OLISV-T0080 · proof.derive

- **English lemma / sense:** inductive step — The part of an induction proof that derives the property for a constructed object from the induction hypotheses for its immediate components.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** indukcijny krok: The productive -ijny form is intelligible, but indukcijski preserves the adjective family already frozen in OLISV-T0146 and aligns directly with BCS technical morphology.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** East and South Slavic traditions do not share the selected krok head uniformly.; Both indukcijski and indukcijny are morphologically transparent; the choice is an edition consistency policy.

## OLISV-T0379 · proof.mathematical-induction

- **English lemma / sense:** mathematical induction — The ordinary induction proof principle with a base case at zero and a successor step from the case n to n+1; it is distinct from complete or strong induction and from an induction axiom schema as a theory component.

- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Matematična indukcija combines the official adjective with the international noun used consistently across the direct Ukrainian, Polish, and Croatian sources. The sense is deliberately limited to ordinary base-plus-successor induction; strong induction and an axiom schema remain separate canon concepts.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** polna indukcija: This names complete or strong induction in some Slavic traditions and would misclassify the ordinary proof in OLP-0193.; indukcijska metoda: A descriptive phrase is possible, but it obscures the standard specialist head directly shared across Ukrainian, Polish, and Croatian sources.

## OLISV-T0245 · proof.recursion

- **English lemma / sense:** recursion — The method defining a function successively from a base value and a successor clause.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `rekursija`; lemma `rekursija`; feminine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive rekursije; use obyčajna rekursija and rekursivno opreděliti where an adjective is needed.
- **Kirilična forma:** `рекурсија`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Rekursija is the broadly recognizable international mathematical noun and matches the source's base-and-successor construction. It is provisional because the bounded dictionary snapshot contains no direct recursion row.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.74 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0245-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: рекурсия; uk: рекурсія; pl: rekurencja; cs: rekurze; bcs: rekurzija; рекурзија; bg: рекурсия.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=17215; lemma=čislo; gloss=number); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** povtorno opreděljenje: An explanatory paraphrase, not the standard mathematical method name.; indukcija: Induction proves properties; it does not define the function in this passage.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Polish and Czech prefer their own established cognates; the international form is chosen for cross-Slavic recognition.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0180 · proof.redundant-axiom

- **English lemma / sense:** redundant axiom — An axiom that is already a semantic consequence of the other axioms in the system and can therefore be removed without changing semantic closure.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izlišnja aksioma`; lemma `izlišnja aksioma`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The feminine adjective izlišnja agrees with aksioma; use genitive izlišnjoj aksiomy and plural izlišnje aksiomy.
- **Kirilična forma:** `излишња аксиома`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Izlišnja aksioma uses the frozen adjective that explicitly includes redundant in its gloss and is especially transparent to South Slavic and Bulgarian readers. Open Logic's formula supplies the formal meaning and prevents the ordinary adjective from remaining vague.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.83 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0180-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: избыточная аксиома; uk: зайва аксіома; pl: zbędny aksjomat; cs: nadbytečný axiom; bcs: suvišan aksiom; bg: излишна аксиома.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16607]; dictionary_id=27228; lemma=izlišnji, izlišny; gloss=superfluous, redundant, excess); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1498]; dictionary_id=24180; lemma=aksioma; gloss=axiom); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** izbytočna aksioma: The izbyt- family is strong in Russian and West Slavic, but the separately frozen izlišny row directly includes redundant, reaches South Slavic and Bulgarian more transparently, and is selected for the edition.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** National terminology divides between izbyt-/nadbyt- and izliš-/suviš- adjective families.

## OLISV-T0175 · proof.semantic-deduction-theorem

- **English lemma / sense:** semantic deduction theorem — The semantic equivalence between entailment of B from Gamma together with A and entailment of the conditional A implies B from Gamma alone.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `semantična teorema dedukcije`; lemma `semantična teorema dedukcije`; feminine named-theorem noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive semantičnoj teoremy dedukcije. Keep semantična explicit: the result concerns semantic entailment, not a derivability rule inside a proof calculus.
- **Kirilična forma:** `семантична теорема дедукције`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Semantična teorema dedukcije transparently composes the already canonical semantic and deduction families and follows the edition's established feminine teorema naming policy. The explicit semantic modifier prevents confusion with the syntactic deduction theorem, while the dedicated canon ID pins the exact entailment biconditional.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.92 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0175-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: семантическая теорема о дедукции; uk: семантична теорема дедукції; pl: semantyczne twierdzenie o dedukcji; cs: sémantická věta o dedukci; bcs: semantički teorem dedukcije; семантичка теорема дедукције; bg: семантична теорема за дедукцията.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[15062]; dictionary_id=35935; lemma=semantika; gloss=semantics); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[4819]; dictionary_id=25162; lemma=dedukcija; gloss=deduction); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** semantičny teorem dedukcije: The masculine South-Slavic-facing variant is intelligible, but it conflicts with the edition's established feminine teorema construction for named mathematical results.; dedukcijska teorema: Without semantična this can denote a theorem internal to a deduction calculus and therefore erases the source's semantic/syntactic boundary.; teorema semantičnogo slědovanja: This describes the surrounding consequence relation but suppresses the recognized deduction-theorem identity and its bridge from an added premise to implication.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No frozen Interslavic dictionary row directly attests the complete named theorem.; National traditions vary between theorem nouns and linking constructions; the selected phrase follows the edition's established naming policy.

## OLISV-T0058 · proof.soundness

- **English lemma / sense:** soundness — The property that a proof system derives only semantically valid consequences.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `korektnost`; lemma `korektnost`; noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine abstract noun; use korektny for the corresponding adjective and teorema korektnosti for the theorem.
- **Kirilična forma:** `коректност`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Korektnost matches the established mathematical sense in several Slavic traditions and avoids the misleading literal calque of English soundness as an acoustic or physical property.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.81 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0058-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: корректность; uk: коректність; pl: poprawność; cs: korektnost; bcs: korektnost; bg: коректност.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** zdravost: A literal calque of English soundness suggests health or physical integrity and obscures the semantic-preservation theorem.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Polish specialist usage prefers poprawność, so the selected international form is not equally native-looking across all Slavic readerships.

## OLISV-T0152 · proof.strong-induction

- **English lemma / sense:** strong induction — Induction on a natural-number index whose step may use the claim for every strictly smaller index.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `silna indukcija`; lemma `silna indukcija`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive silnoj indukcije; in this unit the induction parameter is the final index of a formation sequence, not its ordinary length.
- **Kirilična forma:** `силна индукција`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Silna indukcija directly mirrors the source label and converges across the East-Slavic and Bulgarian comparanda while remaining transparent elsewhere. It also prevents confusion with strukturna indukcija, which the preceding proof applies to formula construction rather than to a numerical index.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.84 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0152-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: сильная индукция; uk: сильна індукція; pl: silna indukcja; cs: silná indukce; bcs: jaka indukcija; јака индукција; bg: силна индукция.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[16423]; dictionary_id=1310); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** polna indukcija: This West-Slavic synonym family is recognizable, but it obscures the source's explicit strong label and risks collision with the canon's separate polnota terminology.; strukturna indukcija: That canonical term applies to induction over generated syntax; the present proof is numerical induction on the final sequence index.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** West-Slavic textbooks may prefer complete-induction terminology, while South-Slavic sources may use jaka rather than silna.; The source proof confuses final index and sequence length; the translation states the valid final-index induction explicitly.; No official or native certification is claimed for the complete specialist compound.

## OLISV-T0055 · proof.tableau

- **English lemma / sense:** tableau proof — A tree of signed formulas generated by inference rules and used to test whether every branch closes.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `tabelarny dokaz`; lemma `tabelarny dokaz`; noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine noun phrase; tabelarny agrees with dokaz. The source macro tableau remains unchanged where the build system requires it.
- **Kirilična forma:** `табеларны доказ`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Tabelarny transparently connects the method to a table-like branching display, while dokaz keeps the practical proof function explicit for Slavic readers who may not know the borrowed French-English label tableau.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.64 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0055-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: табличное доказательство; uk: табличний доказ; pl: dowód tablicowy; cs: tabulkový důkaz; bcs: tablični dokaz; bg: таблично доказателство.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** tableau: The unadapted loan is opaque to many Slavic readers and is retained only as a protected source macro where technically required.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The national traditions distribute this concept across table, tableau, and semantic-tree terminology; the edition compound prioritizes transparent function over one national convention.

## OLISV-T0360 · relation.dense-order

- **English lemma / sense:** dense order — An order in which, whenever x is strictly below y, some z lies strictly between x and y.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `gųsty poredok`; lemma `gųsty poredok`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.

- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: функциональное отношение; uk: функціональне відношення; pl: relacja funkcyjna; cs: funkční relace; bcs: funkcionalna relacija; bg: функционално отношение.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** funkcijno odnošenje: Funkcijno can be understood compositionally, but it is less standard for the technical right-unique property and conflicts with the accepted funkcionalno precedent.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** This property is right-uniqueness only; totality is a separate canon entry and must not be inferred from functional alone.

## OLISV-T0006 · relation.mathematical

- **English lemma / sense:** relation — A set of ordered tuples, especially a binary relation represented as a subset of a Cartesian square.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** relacija: Relacija is well established in the inherited mathematics and in West/South comparanda, but it has no direct lemma row in the bounded dictionary data; retain it as a searchable alternative.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Specialist usage may favor relacija in some contexts; this seed chooses the directly dictionary-backed Slavic-rooted lemma.

## OLISV-T0362 · relation.order-endpoint

- **English lemma / sense:** endpoint of a linear order — A least or greatest element of a linear order; not an arbitrary minimal or maximal element of a non-linear order.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `krajny element poredka`; lemma `krajny element poredka`; masculine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive plural krajnyh elementov poredka. In definitions, identify the pair as najmenši i največši element to prevent confusion with non-linear minimal/maximal elements.
- **Kirilična forma:** `крајны елемент поредка`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Krajny is an official Interslavic dictionary lemma meaning extreme or utmost and yields the concise recurring phrase bez krajnyh elementov. Because national mathematical sources often prefer an explicit least/greatest formulation, the house usage always binds the term to najmenši i največši element at first definition. The definition is restricted to linear orders, where least/minimal and greatest/maximal coincide.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.79 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0362-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: концевая точка линейного порядка; uk: найменший або найбільший елемент порядку; pl: element najmniejszy lub największy; cs: nejmenší nebo největší prvek uspořádání; bcs: najmanji ili najveći element; bg: най-малък или най-голям елемент на наредбата.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=757, krajny, gloss extreme/utmost; inflection row krajnyh); OL-MODEL-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER (lines 19-22); RU-KFU-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2013 (physical PDF page 63, printed page 62); PL-PW-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2022 (physical/printed PDF page 23); HR-ZAGREB-DENSE-LINEAR-ORDER-2015 (physical PDF page 52, printed page 48); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/dist/index.browser.min.js; isv-Cyrl)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** konec poredka: Konec can suggest temporal termination or a physical end and does not transparently denote an element of the ordered domain.; graničny element: Boundary terminology risks a metric or topological reading and lacks the direct dictionary-plus-definition support of krajny element.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete specialist phrase is synthesized rather than directly certified by an official Interslavic mathematical dictionary.; The concise head is safe only with the recorded linear-order sense and first-use least/greatest gloss.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** teorija skupov: Skup is prominent in part of South Slavic usage, but množstvo is the established lane-wide mathematical-set head and aligns more broadly with the East and West Slavic comparison set.

## OLISV-T0217 · set.cantor-theorem

- **English lemma / sense:** Cantor's theorem — The theorem instantiated here as the nonexistence of an injection from the power set of X into X.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Kantorova teorema`; lemma `Kantorova teorema`; feminine proper-name possessive phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine adjective-noun agreement; genitive Kantorovoj teoremy. Keep initial K in both scripts.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Канторова теорема`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Kantorova preserves the internationally recognized name while ensuring deterministic Cyrillic yields Канторова. The accepted predecessor spells the Latin head Cantorova; this continuation deliberately normalizes only its initial letter for script stability while retaining the same no-injection sense.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0217-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: теорема Кантора; uk: теорема Кантора; pl: twierdzenie Cantora; cs: Cantorova věta; bcs: Cantorov teorem; bg: теорема на Кантор.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** Cantorova teorema: Latin C would deterministically project to Cyrillic Ц, producing the misleading form Цанторова; the canonical K spelling is script-stable and matches East and Bulgarian usage.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Polish, Czech, and BCS conventionally retain Latin C; the K spelling is a deliberate bilingual-script normalization, not a claim that those national spellings are wrong.

- **Alternativy / alternatives:** nesčisljeno množstvo: The directly attested adjective means countless or innumerable and can function as quantitative hyperbole; it does not precisely negate the selected enumerable predicate.; neizčislimo množstvo: This would negate canonical izčislimy in the computability sense and therefore mean noncomputable rather than nonenumerable.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The adjective is an edition-level derivation from inherited izčisljivo, not a direct dictionary lemma.; National specialist comparanda are editorially normalized and not independently certified by the bounded dictionary.

## OLISV-T0213 · set.ordered-pair

- **English lemma / sense:** ordered pair — A two-place ordered object represented in this source by the Kuratowski set {{x},{x,y}}; the representation is not claimed to be unique.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `uporędžena para`; lemma `uporędžena para`; feminine noun phrase.

- **Kirilična forma:** `упореджена пара`

- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: упорядоченная пара; uk: впорядкована пара; pl: para uporządkowana; cs: uspořádaná dvojice; bcs: uređeni par; bg: наредена двойка.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[4323]; dictionary_id=2257; lemma=para; gloss=pair); OL-SET-PAIRS-PRODUCTS-ISV (lines 10-37); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** uređena para: The South Slavic form is concise but narrower; uporędžena is already the accepted project form and is more directly recognizable to East and West Slavic readers.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The displayed Kuratowski encoding is one representation among several; the term denotes the ordered object, not uniquely that encoding.

## OLISV-T0212 · set.power-set

- **English lemma / sense:** power set — For a set X, the set whose elements are exactly all subsets of X.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `množstvo vsih podmnožstv`; lemma `množstvo vsih podmnožstv`; neuter descriptive noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter head; the domain follows as a genitive complement, e.g. množstvo vsih podmnožstv množstva X.
- **Kirilična forma:** `множство всих подмножств`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The descriptive head exposes the defining property to readers across Slavic traditions and is already established in an accepted set-theory unit. It avoids making comprehension depend on national potęgowy/potenční or Boolean-set conventions.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.96 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0212-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: множество всех подмножеств; булеан; uk: множина всіх підмножин; булеан; pl: zbiór potęgowy; cs: potenční množina; bcs: skup svih podskupova; partitivni skup; bg: множество на всички подмножества; булеан.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[15156,16499]; dictionary_id=36434,36456; lemma=množstvo,podmnožstvo); OL-SET-SUBSETS-ISV (lines 79-84); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** potencijalno množstvo: The international adjective follows West Slavic usage but can read as merely potential and is less transparent than the exact all-subsets definition.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Bulean and national power/potential adjectives remain useful aliases; the canon selects the maximally transparent descriptive form.

## OLISV-T0247 · set.proper-subset

- **English lemma / sense:** proper subset — A subset that is not equal to the set containing it.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `vlastno podmnožstvo`; lemma `vlastno podmnožstvo`; neuter adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Genitive vlastnogo podmnožstva; use A jest vlastnym podmnožstvom B for the strict inclusion relation.
- **Kirilična forma:** `властно подмножство`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The direct mathematical subset row anchors the head, while vlastno states the strictness required by the source. This keeps the Dedekind criterion distinct from an arbitrary subset.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.88 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0247-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: собственное подмножество; uk: власна підмножина; pl: podzbiór właściwy; cs: vlastní podmnožina; bcs: pravo podskup; прави подскуп; bg: същинско подмножество.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=36456; lemma=podmnožstvo; gloss=subset (mathematics)); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** strogy podmnožstvo: Strogy is already used for strict syntactic subforms and is less idiomatic for set inclusion.; podmnožstvo s konečnym dopolnjenjem: Describes a cofinite subset, not every proper subset.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The complete phrase is compositional; national mathematical adjectives vary.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0191 · set.pure

- **English lemma / sense:** pure set — A set whose elements, recursively, are themselves sets, so that its transitive formation contains no urelements.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `čisto množstvo`; lemma `čisto množstvo`; neuter noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** The neuter adjective agrees with množstvo: genitive čistogo množstva; plural čista množstva.
- **Kirilična forma:** `чисто множство`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Čisto množstvo is a direct componential match across the six comparison traditions, and the current source supplies the recursive no-urelement boundary.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0191-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: чистое множество; uk: чиста множина; pl: zbiór czysty; cs: čistá množina; bcs: čisti skup; чисти скуп; bg: чисто множество.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[3445]; dictionary_id=582; lemma=čisty; gloss=clean, pure); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[15156]; dictionary_id=36434; lemma=množstvo; gloss=set (mathematics)); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** bezurelementno množstvo: The negative compound makes one consequence explicit but is cumbersome and does not express the established recursive pure-set term as directly.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The informal source explanation tacitly assumes well-founded formation and does not itself distinguish non-well-founded pure sets.

## OLISV-T0218 · set.russell-paradox

- **English lemma / sense:** Russell's paradox — The contradiction produced by unrestricted comprehension applied to the property of not belonging to oneself.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Russellov paradoks`; lemma `Russellov paradoks`; masculine proper-name possessive phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine phrase; genitive Russellovogo paradoksa. Preserve the internationally established double l in the proper name.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Русселлов парадокс`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The possessive form is already established in the edition and mirrors Czech/BCS morphology while retaining the proper name recognizable across all comparison languages.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.97 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0218-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: парадокс Рассела; uk: парадокс Рассела; pl: paradoks Russella; cs: Russellův paradox; bcs: Russellov paradoks; bg: парадокс на Ръсел.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[12428]; dictionary_id=543; lemma=paradoks; gloss=paradox); OL-SET-RUSSELL-ISV (lines 11-80); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** paradoks Russella: The genitive construction is valid and common nationally, but the possessive form is the existing project head and produces a more compact stable label.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The paradox depends on unrestricted comprehension; it must not be attributed to the restricted separation schema recorded separately.

## OLISV-T0219 · set.separation-schema

- **English lemma / sense:** separation schema — The restricted comprehension schema asserting, for a prior set z and formula A, the existence of the subset of z satisfying A.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `shema vyděljenja`; lemma `shema vyděljenja`; feminine noun phrase with genitive singular.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine head shema with genitive verbal noun vyděljenja; genitive shemy vyděljenja.
- **Kirilična forma:** `схема выдєљења`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Shema is directly frozen. The selected vyděljenje is an editorial specialist normalization supported by the displayed restricted-comprehension formula and Russian, Ukrainian, and Czech convergence; the frozen odděliti row is adverse generic comparison, not direct support for this stem. Shema records that the displayed form varies with formulas even though the English prose calls it a separation principle.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.86 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0219-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: схема выделения; uk: схема виділення; pl: schemat aksjomatu wyróżniania; schemat separacji; cs: schéma vydělení; bcs: aksiomska shema separacije; shema izdvajanja; bg: схема на отделянето.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14890]; dictionary_id=18814; lemma=shema; gloss=scheme); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[15104]; dictionary_id=2951; lemma=odděliti; gloss=separate); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** shema separacije: The internationalism is familiar in Polish and BCS specialist usage, but vyděljenje is more directly parsable across the intended audience and better matches the East/West specialist comparison; the frozen generic comparator does not decide between the stems.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The English prose calls this the separation principle, whereas the canonical head says schema because the displayed formula is schematic; the source also omits explicit parameter and freshness conditions for A(x), which the canon does not silently supply.

## OLISV-T0003 · set.subset

- **English lemma / sense:** subset — A set all of whose elements belong to another set.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `podmnožstvo`; lemma `podmnožstvo`; neuter noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Neuter noun; genitive singular podmnožstva, plural podmnožstva. Use A jest podmnožstvom B for A ⊆ B.
- **Kirilična forma:** `подмножство`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The compound transparently inherits množstvo, is explicitly tagged for mathematics in the dictionary, and occurs with the same sense in the inherited corpus.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.99 (high); `seed_preferred`; revision `OLISV-T0003-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: подмножество; uk: підмножина; pl: podzbiór; cs: podmnožina; bcs: podskup; bg: подмножество.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260824 (wordList[16499]; dictionary_id=36456); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** podgrupa: A subgroup is only a special kind of subset and cannot serve as the general set-theoretic term.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No specific uncertainty beyond the provisional edition boundary.

## OLISV-T0004 · set.union

- **English lemma / sense:** union; union over a set — The set containing exactly the elements belonging to at least one member of a specified family; this includes binary union and the operation denoted by union X.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `unija množstv`; lemma `unija`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine noun; genitive unije. Use unija množstv for the general operation, unija X or formula notation for the union over a set X, and unija A i B for binary union.
- **Kirilična forma:** `унија множств`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The established international head remains unchanged. OLP-0170 explicitly introduces the family operation union X, so the revision broadens the recorded sense without conflating it with intersection or with the mereological sum introduced later in the same unit.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.72 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0004-r0002`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: объединение множеств; uk: об'єднання множин; pl: suma zbiorów; cs: sjednocení množin; bcs: unija skupova; bg: обединение на множества.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260824 (wordList[18009]; dictionary_id=13296); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260824 (wordList[15156]; dictionary_id=36434); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** sjedinjenje množstv: This Slavic-rooted alternative is semantically attractive and Noether attests sjedinenje, but no direct noun row or set-union attestation is present in the bounded official snapshot.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** The choice between unija množstv and sjedinenje množstv needs stronger specialist Interslavic attestation; no human review is required to use this reversible provisional seed.

## OLISV-T0192 · set.urelement

- **English lemma / sense:** urelement — An object admitted as an element of sets while not itself being a set.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `urelement`; lemma `urelement`; masculine noun.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Masculine noun; genitive urelementa, plural urelementy. Use only for a non-set object in a set-theoretic universe.
- **Kirilična forma:** `урелемент`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** Urelement is the compact international technical term used by the source and is directly reconstructible from the established element head. Keeping it distinct avoids overloading atom, which has additional logical and mereological senses.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.73 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0192-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: урэлемент; uk: урелемент; pl: urelement; cs: urelement; bcs: urelement; урелемент; bg: урелемент.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6006]; dictionary_id=17372; lemma=element; gloss=element); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** praelement: The calque is morphologically transparent but is not the source term and is less stable across specialist traditions than urelement.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** No exact urelement entry occurs in the frozen dictionary response.; Atom is deliberately rejected because it is ambiguous across set theory, logic, physics, and mereology.

## OLISV-T0386 · theory.weaker-theory

- **English lemma / sense:** weaker theory — A theory with fewer deductive consequences than a comparison theory; in this unit Robinson Q does not prove commutativity of addition and therefore has models excluded by true arithmetic and Peano arithmetic.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `slabša teorija`; lemma `slabša teorija`; feminine comparative adjective-noun phrase.
- **Upotrěbljenje / inflection:** Feminine comparative slabša agrees with teorija; use od plus genitive for the comparison, as in slabša od teorije TA.
- **Kirilična forma:** `слабша теорија`
- **Argumentacija / rationale:** The comparative slabša is already the accepted canon morphology in OLISV-T0268, while the Russian and Croatian sources independently attest the exact theory-strength contrast. The phrase remains relational: it does not mean that Q is absolutely weak for every metamathematical purpose.
- **Uvěrjenost / status:** 0.95 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0386-r0001`.
- **Slovjanske sravnenja / comparanda:** ru: более слабая теория; uk: слабша теорія; pl: słabsza teoria; cs: slabší teorie; bcs: slabija teorija; bg: по-слаба теория.
- **Dokazy / evidence:** OL-MODEL-ARITHMETIC-MODELS-Q (lines 13-23); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[18597]; dictionary_id=1341; lemma=slaby; gloss=weak, feeble); RU-MI-RAS-PROVABILITY-ARITHMETIC-2020 (physical page 2 / extracted lines 44-52); HR-ZAGREB-GODEL-INCOMPLETENESS-2011 (physical page 2 / extracted lines 96-103); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; standard Cyrillic, standard flavorization)
- **Alternativy / alternatives:** slaba teorija: The positive adjective loses the explicit comparison to TA and PA made in this unit.; malo silna teorija: This analytic phrase is less compact and lacks the official weak-adjective precedent.
- **Neizvěstnost / uncertainty:** Theory strength is relative to a comparison theory and proof system; the short phrase relies on the local TA/PA contrast.

## Pravilo reprodukcije · Reproduction rule

Replay `CANON_INDEX.json` po porędku, prověri každy byte/hash, grupuj zapisy po `entry_id` i izberi najvyššu nepretrganu aktivnu reviziju. Nikogda ne prěpisuj historiju: dodavaj naslědnik s jasnym supersession, dokazami i neizvěstnostju.

Replay `CANON_INDEX.json` in order, verify every byte/hash pin, group by `entry_id`, and select the greatest contiguous active revision. Never overwrite history: append a successor with explicit supersession, evidence and uncertainty.

Granica tvrđenja / claim boundary: reverzibilna projektna terminologija, ne oficialna standardizacija, rodnojezyčna certifikacija, naučna recenzija ili dokončenje vsih 722 jedinic.
